### [1] [The SymTFT and Quantum Phases of Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21715)
*Salvatore D. Pace,Sakura Schafer-Nameki,Carolyn Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于对称性拓扑场论（SymTFT）框架的教学性综述，系统介绍其在量子物相中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通对称与广义对称的日益丰富的分类需要一个统一描述框架，将对称性数据与具体量子系统的细节解耦。

Method: 在高一维的拓扑场论中编码对称性，构建SymTFT框架；对 (1+1) 维融合范畴对称性，阐述如何借助SymTFT刻画反常、分类有能隙相并组织无能隙相的对称结构。

Result: 给出SymTFT的教学性系统介绍，并综述其向高维对称性、连续对称性、费米子系统、混合态及时空对称性等方面的推广。

Conclusion: SymTFT为普通与广义对称性提供统一语言，是有能隙与无能隙物相分类及反常分析的强有力工具，且具有广泛的推广空间。

Abstract: The symmetry topological field theory (SymTFT) provides a unified framework for ordinary and generalized symmetries of quantum many-body systems. By encoding symmetry in a topological theory in one higher dimension, it separates symmetry data from the details of a specific quantum system. In this review, we give a pedagogical introduction to this framework and survey its applications to phases of quantum matter. For fusion category symmetries in ${(1+1)}$d, we explain how the SymTFT characterizes anomalies, classifies gapped phases, and organizes the symmetry structure of gapless phases. We also give a broad overview of further developments of this framework, including extensions to symmetries in higher dimensions, continuous symmetries, fermionic systems, mixed states, and spacetime symmetries.

</details>


### [2] [Constraints on the volume enclosed by Fermi surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21720)
*Subir Sachdev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本综述将Luttinger定理推广到量子自旋液体的parton规范理论与分数化费米液体(FL*)，证明FL*的费米体积对应密度移位一个量子化量（通常为ρ−1），并讨论Oshikawa磁通插入论证及其向FL*与无能隙自旋液体的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米液体的基本性质是费米面体积由总电子密度ρ决定；而FL*中费米面体积对应的是ρ经量子化移位后的表达式。该移位与量子自旋液体的对称性分数化量子化数据相关，但需要系统论证其来源。

Method: 将原始Luttinger对FL的证明推广至量子自旋液体与FL*的parton规范理论，从而确立FL*的费米体积；并梳理Oshikawa通量插入论证在FL中的形式及其向FL*（含无能隙自旋液体情形）的推广。

Result: 在parton规范理论框架下验证了FL*的费米体积满足移位后的Luttinger计数，移位量由自旋液体的量子化对称性分数化数据确定（最常见为ρ−1，假定自旋旋转不变）。

Conclusion: FL*的费米体积计数是FL Luttinger定理与自旋液体分数化数据的结合，磁通插入论证同样适用，为FL*相提供一致性判据。

Abstract: A fundamental property of a Fermi liquid (FL) is that the volume enclosed by the Fermi surface is determined by $ρ$, the total density of electrons. In a fractionalized Fermi liquid (FL*), the volume enclosed by the Fermi surface is determined by the same expression after $ρ$ has been shifted by a quantized amount, most commonly to $ρ-1$ (we assume spin rotation invariance, for simplicity). The FL* shift is related to the quantized symmetry fractionalization data of a quantum spin liquid, whose fractionalized spinon excitations are present alongside the Fermi surface quasiparticles. In this review article, we establish the FL* Fermi volume by generalizing the original Luttinger proof for FL to parton gauge theories of the quantum spin liquid and FL*. We also discuss Oshikawa's flux insertion arguments for FL, and their extension to FL*, including a discussion of gapless spin liquids.

</details>


### [3] [Tunable Topological Charge Order in Magnetic Moiré Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21745)
*Conor Smith,Yubo Yang,Shiwei Zhang,Miguel A. Morales*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用神经网络量子态（NQS）方法，作者绘制了moiré超晶格中ν_B=1（每个moiré原胞三个电子）体系的基态相图，揭示量子霍尔液体与电子晶体之间存在一个"分子电荷序与量子化霍尔响应共存"的扩展拓扑分子区间，并发现蜂窝点处doublon关联随相互作用增强最终形成Néel型电荷密度波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁场下的二维电子存在两种看似不相容的基态——具有量子化霍尔电导的不可压缩量子霍尔液体，与相互作用驱动电荷有序的电子晶体。TMD双层moiré超晶格提供了调控二者竞争的平台，但该竞争的完整基态相图及中间态性质缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 采用神经网络量子态（NQS）并结合多项方法学改进，计算ν_B=1、每个moiré原胞含三个电子时，在moiré超晶格势场中的基态相图；分析从深度三角势极限（三电子人造原子构成的Wigner分子晶体）到浅势的演化；在蜂窝点（两个简并moiré势阱，单/双占位产生涌现Ising自由度）研究doublon关联与对称性破缺。

Result: 两个端点之间被一个扩展区间分隔，其中分子电荷序与量子化霍尔响应共存；深度三角势下得到拓扑平庸的三电子Wigner分子晶体；随势深降低，分子电荷序保留而霍尔响应出现。蜂窝点处，Chern绝缘体内存在短程doublon关联，关联随相互作用增强而增长，最终破坏子晶格对称性，形成doublon间长程有序的Néel型CDW。该拓扑分子区间位于当前实验可及的扭转角与磁场范围内。

Conclusion: 量子霍尔液体与电子晶体之间的竞争并非简单一级转变，而存在拓扑与分子序融合的中间相；该相及其磁场/扭转角窗口对现有moiré实验可直接探测，所发展的NQS方法为强磁场下关联量子物质研究提供了可扩展框架。

Abstract: Two-dimensional electrons in a strong magnetic field exhibit two seemingly incompatible ground states: incompressible quantum Hall liquids with a quantized Hall conductance, and electron crystals in which interactions drive the charge into ordered patterns. Moiré superlattices in transition-metal-dichalcogenide bilayers offer a tunable arena for this competition. Using neural quantum states (NQS) together with several methodological advances, we show how this competition unfolds. In particular, we demonstrate that the two end points are separated by an extended regime in which molecular charge order coexists with a quantized Hall response. We map the ground-state phase diagram of electrons at Landau-level filling $ν_B=1$ in the presence of a moiré superlattice potential, with three electrons per moiré cell. In the deep triangular potential limit, the electrons form the topologically trivial "Wigner molecular crystal'' of three-electron artificial atoms, a state recently imaged at zero magnetic field in moiré bilayers. As the potential depth is lowered, molecular charge order remains while quantized Hall response emerges. At the honeycomb point, where each cell contains three electrons and two degenerate moiré minima, wells are either singly or doubly occupied and the doublons provide an emergent Ising degree of freedom. Starting from modest interaction strength, we find short-range doublon correlations within the Chern insulator which grow with interaction strength and eventually break sublattice symmetry, forming a Néel CDW with long-range order between doublons. The topological molecular regime lies at twist angles and magnetic fields directly accessible to current experiments. The methodological advances presented provide a scalable framework for exploring a broad range of questions in correlated quantum matter in the presence of magnetic fields.

</details>


### [4] [Coexisting orders and dipolar Luttinger liquids in $\mathbb{Z}_N$ quantum torus chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22046)
*Seunghun Lee,Seungho Lee,Hyun-Yong Lee,Jung Hoon Han*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 建立了1D $\mathbb{Z}_N$量子环面链的统一基态相图，与偶极簇链、偶极Ising链通过对偶相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathbb{Z}_N$量子环面链及其偶极对偶模型（偶极簇链、偶极Ising链）的相图此前缺乏统一描述，尤其是依赖局部Hilbert空间维度$N$及其奇偶性的相结构、以及无能隙偶极激发与长程序共存的可能性未被系统刻画。

Method: 张量网络计算结合玻色化与微扰理论；用涌现的Luttinger液体理论理解dLL相稳定性，用竞争性umklapp与clock各向异性微扰驱动BKT相变；奇数$N$时利用指数简并基态流形内低能动力学映射为有效XXZ链。

Result: 识别出$X$与$Z$长程序共存的$C$相，以及分隔共存相与单序相的偶极Luttinger液体dLL$_{\rm I}$、dLL$_{\rm II}$；dLL中无能隙偶极激发与长程序共存。$C$与dLL相均在$N\geq5$出现，但$N\to\infty$仅$C$相存活。奇数$N\geq5$时在$\theta=\pi$附近出现第三dLL$_{\rm III}$相。

Conclusion: 给出由$N$及奇偶性主导的丰富统一相图；对偶性考虑暗示偶极簇链中可能存在无能隙偶极对称保护拓扑相与自发偶极对称破缺。

Abstract: We establish a unified ground-state phase diagram for the one-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_N$ quantum torus chain, which is connected to dipolar cluster and dipolar Ising chains via noninvertible dualities. Combining tensor-network calculations with bosonization and perturbation theory, we establish a rich phase diagram governed by the local Hilbert-space dimension $N$ and its parity. We identify phases with coexisting $X$ and $Z$ long-range orders ($C$ phase), and dipolar Luttinger liquids (dLL$_{\rm I}$ and dLL$_{\rm II}$) separating coexisting phases from single-order phases. Gapless dipolar excitations coexist with long-range order in dLLs. Both the $C$ and dLL phases appear at $N \geq 5$, but only the $C$ phases persist as $N\to \infty$. The stability of the dLL phase is understood by an emergent Luttinger liquid theory, with Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions out of it driven by competing umklapp and clock anisotropy perturbations. For odd $N \geq 5$, a third dLL phase (dLL$_{\rm III}$) emerges near $θ= π$ due to the low-energy dynamics inside the exponentially degenerate ground-state manifold that maps onto an effective XXZ chain. Duality considerations suggest a possible gapless dipolar symmetry-protected topological phase and spontaneous dipole-symmetry breaking in dipolar cluster chains.

</details>


### [5] [Orbital differentiation in a rare earth ion near half filling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22462)
*D. W. Tam,Ø. S. Fjellvåg,B. Gonano,E. X. M. Trewick,M. Månsson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用Quanty对岩盐结构SmN进行全多重态计算，其结果与磁化率、非弹性中子散射及DFT自旋密度一致；通过调节晶场参数发现基态J=5/2多重态发生轨道分化，形成两个轨道刚度不同的子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土离子电子结构细节对理解关联态起源至关重要，但现有基于波函数的方法（如CASSCF）计算昂贵且无法用于金属体系，需寻找快速廉价的替代方案。

Method: 使用晶场软件Quanty对立方岩盐结构SmN开展全多重态（full multiplet）计算，并将结果与磁化率、非弹性中子散射实验及DFT自旋密度预测进行对比；同时调节晶场参数以考察基态波函数结构。

Result: 计算得到的可观测量与磁化率及非弹性中子散射实验精确吻合，空间分辨自旋密度也与DFT预测一致；调节晶场参数后，基态J=5/2多重态分化为两个具有不同轨道刚度的独立子空间，表现为对Hund规则的违背。

Conclusion: 全多重态方法可在快速廉价的计算框架下揭示稀土材料中的轨道分化等微观细节，为稀土材料微观模型提供支持，并可能为SmN中非常规三重态超导性的主张提供依据。

Abstract: In the electronic structure of rare earth ions, details can be crucial for understanding the origin of correlated states. Currently, state-of-the-art wavefunction-based methods (e.g. CASSCF) can capture single-ion properties of molecular magnets and some band insulating systems, but are expensive to perform, and cannot be applied to metallic systems. Here, we carry out full multiplet calculations for SmN with the cubic rock-salt structure using the crystal field software Quanty. The calculated experimental observables from our model agree precisely with magnetic susceptibility and inelastic neutron scattering experiments, and the spatially resolved spin density also agrees with DFT predictions. By tuning the crystal field parameters, our full multiplet-derived wavefunctions reveal the presence of orbital differentiation of the ground $J=\frac{5}{2}$ manifold into two groups, which are distinct subspaces with different orbital stiffness. This violation of Hund's rule, observed with a fast and inexpensive calculation tool, represents a step forward for microscopic models of rare earth materials, and may provide support for the claim of unconventional triplet superconductivity in SmN.

</details>


### [6] [Preformed excitons in the excitonic density-wave state of monolayer 1$T'$-MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22536)
*Fei Yang,Keyu Wei,Dekun Yang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Yong Zhang,Xuefeng Wang,Fucong Fei,Fengqi Song,Binghai Yan,Xiaoxiang Xi,Junwei Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在1T'-MoTe₂中，Raman观测到密度波序与集体激发在T_c≈80 K以上消失，而单粒子能隙持续到T_g≈100 K，揭示预形成激子的赝能隙区间；理论将其归因于声子型相退相干与费米型配对破坏两种过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子电中性使凝聚相相干性难以直接探测，预形成但相位非相干的激子长期缺乏特征信号与物理图像。

Method: 以动量分离电子/空穴费米口袋体系中相干配对驱动的激子密度波作为相干性探针；对单层1T'-MoTe₂进行Raman谱、输运与隧穿测量；并构建自洽微观理论，区分玻色子驱动的相位退相干与费米子驱动的配对破坏。

Result: 密度波序及涌现集体激发在T_c≈80 K以上消失，而单粒子能隙延续至T_g≈100 K，形成赝能隙式中间区；低能集体激发被识别为晶格钉扎的phason（相位模），其热致能隙软化增强相位涨落，导致T_c以上相干性丧失；费米准粒子热激发在更高T_g处逐步压制并最终破坏配对振幅。自洽计算定量复现主要观测。

Conclusion: 激子绝缘体中存在预形成配对物理的激子实现，T_c与T_g的分离由相位退相干与配对破坏两过程决定。

Abstract: Phase-incoherent preformed excitons have long been anticipated in excitonic insulators, yet their characteristic signatures and underlying physics remain elusive, as the electrical neutrality of excitons hinders direct probes of condensate phase coherence. Here we exploit the emergent excitonic density wave driven by phase-coherent pairings in systems hosting momentum-separated electron and hole Fermi pockets, such as monolayer 1$T'$-MoTe$_2$, as an experimentally accessible probe of excitonic phase coherence. We observe in 1$T'$-MoTe$_2$ that the density-wave order and its associated emergent collective excitations, as revealed by Raman spectroscopy, vanish above $T_c\approx80~$K, whereas the excitonic-insulator state, characterized by a single-particle gap in transport and tunneling measurements, persists up to $T_g\approx100~$K, revealing an intermediate pseudogap-like regime of preformed excitons without long-range phase coherence. Theoretically, we attribute this intriguing separation to two distinct processes governing the loss of excitonic order by its own emergent excitations: boson-driven phase decoherence and fermion-driven pair breaking. Specifically, we identify an emergent low-lying collective excitation as a lattice-pinned phason (phase mode), whose heating-induced gap softening enhances phase fluctuations and drives condensate phase decoherence above $T_c$, while thermal excitations of fermionic quasiparticles gradually suppress and ultimately destroy pairing amplitude at the higher $T_g$. The self-consistent microscopic calculation quantitatively captures our key observations, establishing an excitonic realization of preformed-pairing physics.

</details>


### [7] [Domain walls of a spin-chirality ferroelectric as tunable quantum particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22662)
*Aleksandr Buzdakov,Atharva Hingane,Sergey Artyukhin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 用DMRG研究阻挫spin-1/2 XXZ链中的手性畴壁，证明在二聚化δ打开自旋能隙后，畴壁成为量子粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有竞争最近邻与次近邻交换的阻挫自旋链中，矢量自旋手性决定电极化，因此铁电畴壁即手性畴壁。半经典图像暗示由于自旋的拓扑约束，畴壁切换动力学是非局域的，但量子自旋链中这类畴壁的性质几乎未知。

Method: 对存在键交替δ、打开自旋能隙的阻挫spin-1/2 XXZ链进行密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算，分析有限链与无限链中手性畴壁的能级、波函数与质量行为。

Result: 有限链中畴壁具有"盒中粒子"的能级与波函数，并在电场下如受恒力粒子般运动；无限链中其质量随δ减小增大约两个数量级以上，并在δ*≈0.012处发散，此处能带底部变平；δ*以下能带出现两个偏离中心简并极小值。大质量源于面内与面外交换能的近乎抵消。

Conclusion: 阻挫自旋链中的手性（铁电）畴壁在量子情形下可被有效描述为具有可调质量的量子粒子，其质量在临界δ*处发散，标志着能带结构的拓扑性转变。

Abstract: In frustrated spin chains with competing nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor exchange, the vector spin chirality sets the electric polarization, so a ferroelectric domain wall is a domain wall of the chirality. The semiclassical picture suggests nonlocal switching dynamics due to topological constraints on the spins, but little is known about such domain walls in a quantum spin chain. Using density-matrix renormalization group calculations for a frustrated spin-1/2 XXZ chain in which a bond alternation $δ$ opens a spin gap, we show that the wall then becomes a quantum particle. In a finite chain, it has the energy levels and wave functions of a particle in a box, and it moves under an electric field as a particle under a constant force does. In the infinite chain, its mass grows by more than two orders of magnitude as $δ$ decreases and diverges at $δ^*\approx0.012$, where the bottom of its band becomes flat; below $δ^*$ the band has two degenerate minima away from its center. The large mass results from a near cancellation of in-plane and out-of-plane exchange energies.

</details>


### [8] [Structural Response to the Hidden Charge Order in Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22864)
*Darren C. Peets,Nikita D. Andriushin,Tobias Ritschel,Morgan C. Allison,Nikolai S. Pavlovskii,Anton A. Kulbakov,Alexandre Ivanov,Jose R. Linares Mardegan,Pablo J. Bereciartua,Sonia Francoual,Jochen Geck,Dmytro S. Inosov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用中子衍射证实中心对称 skyrmion 材料 Sr3Fe2O7 中约 330 K 以下的"隐藏"电荷序伴随明确的结构畸变，即该转变并非单纯的电子重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前通过共振 X 射线散射发现 Sr3Fe2O7 在 ~330 K 以下存在"隐藏"电荷序（电荷歧化与局域化转变），但该手段仅间接探测晶格，无法判断该转变是否具有真实的结构成分，还是仅为电子重排。

Method: 采用对晶格直接敏感的中子散射/衍射测量，对电荷序转变前后的结构演化进行表征，并与既有共振 X 射线散射结果对照。

Result: 中子数据表明，电荷序的出现伴随清晰的结构调制（晶格结构发生可观测的修改）。

Conclusion: Sr3Fe2O7 的电荷有序转变具有真实的结构成分，属电子序与晶格耦合的结构—电荷联合转变，而非单纯的电子密度重排。

Abstract: We previously reported `hidden' charge order below $\sim$330 K in the centrosymmetric skyrmion host Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_7$ [J.-H. Kim et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. 127, 097203 (2021)], finally identifying the charge disproportionation and localization transition that had been first seen in the 1960s. We detected this through resonant x-ray scattering, which directly probes electron density, but is only indirectly sensitive to the lattice structure. This left open the question of whether the charge-ordering transition has a true structural component or consists merely of the reorganization of electrons. Neutrons probe the lattice directly, and we now show, based on neutron data, that the charge order is accompanied by a clear structural modification.

</details>


### [9] [Non-Invertible Quantum Cellular Automata I: Microscopic Definition of Non-Invertible Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22928)
*Sahand Seifnashri,Shu-Heng Shao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出"非可逆量子元胞自动机(QCA)"的概念，即保局域、可对偶且纯的量子信道，并以算子代数方式定义格点量子系统的非可逆对称性，同时构造大量实例并提出实现性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有非可逆对称性研究多依赖拓扑缺陷或张量网络等具体实现，缺乏以"对局域算子作用"为核心的统一、内禀的算子代数刻画，需给出不依赖特定实现的定义框架。

### [12] [Remark on internal relaxation in large number qubit systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21681)
*Martin Janßen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文指出：计算时间 t_c 远小于 Heisenberg 时间 t_H 时系统动力学有效不可逆，量子比特间耦合引入弛豫时间 τ_r；当 t_H ≫ t_c ≳ τ_r 时相干性指数衰减，由此给出比特数阈值 N_c ≈ ln(τ_r/T)，超过该规模须考虑计算过程中的内禀弛豫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大规模量子计算中"计算时间足够短即可视为可逆"这一假设的适用边界，明确多比特系统中不可忽视的内禀退相干/弛豫何时开始影响计算。

Method: 以 Heisenberg 时间 t_H（随 N 指数增长）与计算时间 t_c、弛豫时间 τ_r 的尺度比较为出发点，做量级估计；将系统划分为"被测部分"与"环境部分"，用两者之间的典型跃迁速率的倒数来估计 τ_r，并以单比特典型振荡时间 T 作为能量尺度参考。

Result: 给出阈值标度 N_c ≈ ln(τ_r/T)：N ≫ N_c 时，t_c 虽仍远小于 t_H 却可满足 t_c ≳ τ_r，相干性指数式衰减，计算过程中可能出现显著内禀弛豫。该阈值仅对数依赖 τ_r/T，说明它是较为温和（而非指数级）的规模门槛。

Conclusion: 仅凭 t_c ≪ t_H 不足以保证量子计算的相干可逆性；比特数增长到对数阈值以上后，必须显式计入耦合引起的内禀弛豫。τ_r 可由子系统间跃迁速率估计，为实际器件规模与时间预算提供判据。

Abstract: For a time $t_{c}$ of calculation in an $N$ qubit quantum computer that is much shorter than the intrinsic Heisenberg time $t_H$, $t_c\ll t_H$, the dynamics of the system is effectively irreversible and the couplings between the qubits give rise to a relaxation time scale $τ_r$. If $t_H\gg t_c >\sim τ_r$ one looses quantum coherence exponentially fast. Since $t_H$ scales exponentially with $N$ there is a threshold $N_c$ such that for $N\gg N_c$ one must be aware of possible intrinsic relaxation during calculation. As an order of magnitude estimation we find $N_c\approx \ln(τ_r/T)$ where $T$ is a typical system specific oscillation time of a single qubit. $τ_r$ can be estimated as the typical inverse transition rate between two parts of the system, one of which is treated as an environment of the other.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Gidney and Jones recently introduced ``CSS circuits'', generated by $X$, $Z$, $\MX$, $\MZ$ and CNOT, and showed that they can be sampled with $O(1)$ work per gate. They suspected that the class had been seen before. We point out that it is, up to a normal form, the initialisation--CNOT--measurement (ICM) form introduced in 2015 for compiling fault-tolerant circuits, restricted to $\ket{0}$ and $\ket{+}$ initialisations. The ICM construction answers constructively their question about ``non-CSS-ness as a resource'': every Clifford$+T$ circuit compiles to a CSS circuit acting on $\ket{Y}$ and $\ket{A}$ input states.

</details>

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QEDist 是一个用于认证量子比特 stabilizer 码距离的树搜索方法与软件包，通过稀疏校验、稳定子等价、对称性剪枝及 LP 码结构分解，可在数千量子比特规模上给出精确距离，速度比现有精确求解器快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确距离认证对有限长度量子码的筛选与优化至关重要，但现有精确求解器在大码上计算代价过高，许多 LP 码的距离仅报告为上界。

Method: 基于树搜索，逐量子比特构造候选逻辑算符；利用稀疏奇偶校验、稳定子等价和已验证对称性缩减搜索空间；同时使用已满足与未满足的校验来下界完成逻辑算符所需权重，并对超出剩余预算的分支剪枝；对 lifted-product 码利用乘积结构，将全码搜索部分替换为对经典成分码的较小搜索。

Result: 在单核笔记本上数秒内可为许多码认证高达 20 的精确距离，并为 [[144,12,12]] 码的纠错电路认证故障距离 10；对数千量子比特的 Abelian 与非 Abelian LP 码给出精确距离（此前仅有上界）；相比测试的精确求解器实现数量级加速。基准以 CSS 码为主。

Conclusion: QEDist 使精确距离认证成为选择与优化有限长度量子码的实用工具，且同一形式可推广到非 CSS 码及纠错电路的故障距离。

Abstract: We present QEDist, a method and software package that certifies the distance of qubit stabilizer codes. Building on tree-search methods, QEDist constructs candidate logical operators one qubit at a time, using sparse parity checks, stabilizer equivalence, and verified symmetries to reduce the work. For a cutoff $W$, a completed search either finds a nontrivial logical operator of weight at most $W$ or proves that none exists. Because satisfying one check can violate another, we use both satisfied and unsatisfied checks to lower-bound the weight needed to complete a logical operator, and prune branches whose bound exceeds the remaining budget, which substantially reduces net search time. We benchmark QEDist mainly on Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes; the same formulation extends to non-CSS codes and to the fault distance of syndrome-extraction circuits. For lifted-product (LP) codes, we further exploit the product structure to replace part of the full-code search with a smaller search on a classical constituent. We establish exact distances for codes with up to several thousand qubits, including Abelian and non-Abelian LP codes whose distances were previously reported only as upper bounds. In several seconds of search on one laptop core, QEDist certifies exact distances up to 20 for many of these codes and fault distance 10 for the syndrome-extraction circuit of the $[[144,12,12]]$ code. With orders-of-magnitude speedups over the tested exact solvers, QEDist makes exact distance certification a practical tool for selecting and optimizing finite-length quantum codes.

</details>

Abstract: The python's lunch conjecture relates the complexity of bulk reconstruction in holographic tensor networks to an exponential cost associated with post-selection. We establish a tight query lower bound for this decoding problem in a Haar random oracle model, with a python's lunch circuit that applies Haar random unitary $U$ to a logical input and fixed ancillas, followed by post-selection on $m$ output qubits. Given one copy of the normalized encoded state and coherent oracle access to $U$ and $U^\dagger$, including controlled queries, we prove that recovering even a single logical bit with constant average advantage requires $Ω(2^{m/2})$ queries. Together with the state-independent amplitude amplification upper bound for approximately isometric encodings, this establishes query complexity $Θ(2^{m/2})$ at constant decoding accuracy. We also comment on the decoding cost for other "admissible'" ensembles of unitaries $U$ which give rise to families of approximately isometric holographic maps.

</details>

Abstract: The Schrieffer-Wolff transformation (SWT) is a cornerstone of effective-Hamiltonian theory. However, the SWT is predominantly used for weak-coupling problems, while a systematic counterpart for strongly coupled regimes remains largely unexplored. A general closed-form SWT generator and a clear physical principle for gauge fixing remain elusive, obscuring the transformation's underlying structure. We introduce the dual SWT to deal with the strong-coupling problems. The generalized SWT (gSWT) is formulated for any Hermitian operator and block partition. We establish the geodesic principle for its canonical gauge. Furthermore, we derive an exact closed-form solution of the gSWT generator at every order as a finite nested-commutator sum whose only input is the active gap set. Both the geometric and algebraic aspects of the SWT physics are revealed. The gSWT framework is illustrated in two quantum many-body models, the collective spin model and the transverse-field Ising model.

</details>


### [17] [A Proposal for Quantum Reference Frames in Quantum Field Theory and their Experimental Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21740)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往量子参考系研究多针对有限自由度系统，其在场论情形下的推广尚不清晰；作者希望考察当参考系本身是场论局域模时，关系型可观测量的熵与关联如何随参考系选择而变化，以及场论特性带来何种新效应。

Abstract: Whether a three-dimensional quantum many-body system with geometrically local, bounded-degree interactions can passively protect encoded quantum information has remained a central open question since Kitaev introduced topological quantum computation. Passive protection requires stability against both thermal noise at nonzero temperature and generic static Hamiltonian perturbations Balasubramanian, Davydova, and Lin (BDL) recently established unperturbed three-dimensional thermal self-correction using a recursively defined family of stabilizer Hamiltonians. However, they left open whether their construction remains stable against quantum perturbations, even at zero temperature. In this paper, we show that a slight variant of the BDL code family achieves stability along both boundary axes of the perturbation-temperature phase diagram as the system size grows. Following BDL, in the absence of perturbation it is thermally stable below a critical temperature, achieving a stretched-exponentially long memory lifetime when coupled to any local Markovian thermal bath generated by single-site system-bath interactions satisfying detailed balance with bounded coupling rates. At zero temperature under arbitrary weak, superpolynomially decaying quantum perturbations, the family maintains a uniform bulk spectral gap, superpolynomially small ground-space splitting, and a superpolynomially long closed-system unitary memory lifetime for quasi-locally dressed logical observables. To prove this zero-temperature stability, we establish local topological quantum order across the multiscale hierarchy using a linearly dilated relaxation of the usual Bravyi-Hastings-Michalakis criteria. In the companion paper, we combine the tools developed here with finite-temperature worldline cluster expansions to establish passive self-correction throughout the phase diagram's positive-temperature perturbed interior.

Abstract: Quantum physics forbids cloning of a qubit. Therefore, a qubit is irreversibly lost upon loss of the information carrier. To mitigate this physical loss, it has been proposed to distribute the logical information across an entangled set of physical qubits, some of which can be lost. Previous experiments illustrated this concept, but failed to demonstrate a gain in the information-retrieval efficiency. Here we address this challenge with photons for which the total loss, from generation to detection, is below the loss-tolerance threshold of $50\%$. Using two single-atom emitters in an optical cavity, we encode information into the photons of a graph state and observe an efficiency gain for retrieving quantum correlations between the atoms and the photons for single-qubit measurements in the Pauli $X$ and $Z$ bases. Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) is the one of the most explored application of quantum information theory. While practical QKD systems are being deployed around the world, a major issue of these setups are that one has to trust the measurement devices of both parties. On the other hand, the most secure among such schemes are called device-independent (DI) QKD protocols, where no component of the setup is assumed to be trusted. However, due to their high technological demand, their loophole-free version currently remain out of practical reach. Inspired by the real world, we consider the traditional QKD in a distrustful measurement setting, that is, the measurement devices can only be trusted upto some level of confidence, which might still be large but not completely trusted. We then derive a general lower bound on the asymptotic key rate in this scenario. Then considering perfect detectors and source, and the entanglement-based BB84 (BBM) protocol, we show that to obtain secure keys one needs to trust their devices more than 82 %. However, we observe that such a protocol can not be realised with any level of distrust using the realistic detectors and sources at the current technology. Finally, we also consider the BBM protocol with arbitrary dimensional systems and observe that they are more robust to distrust than qubit systems. Consequently, with the same level of distrust, along with same detector and source efficiences, one can get better key rates with high-dimensional systems.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate how infrared fluctuations and spatial separation shape vacuum entanglement between two finite regions in the relativistic harmonic chain and its Lifshitz generalizations. At fixed lattice spacing and finite geometry, we establish that logarithmic negativity remains uniformly bounded as the mass approaches zero, while mutual information diverges. Infrared-enhanced total correlations therefore do not provide an unbounded distillable-entanglement resource. Using local symplectic consolidation, we identify the collective canonical modes carrying the negativity and follow their evolution with separation. Their participation crosses over from localization near the facing boundaries to a collective distribution within each region. Expansion in the adjacent-state canonical basis connects this evolution to progressively weaker, more extended reference modes, including adjacent-halo directions.

These results refine the ultraviolet mode picture by resolving the long-wavelength organization of the oscillators participating in the surviving entanglement. Across the massless dispersions studied, the normalized participation approaches a universal long-distance profile when separation is scaled by the negativity radius. Its detailed form and the entanglement range nevertheless remain sensitive to ultraviolet deformations. Together, these findings distinguish infrared-enhanced correlations from distillable entanglement and reveal the collective spatial organization underlying its persistence at large separation.

However, designing robust pulse sequences remains challenging in interacting multi-level systems, where multiple independent error channels must be controlled in the presence of experimental imperfections. Here we introduce QORA, a multi-agent artificial intelligence framework that integrates theoretical reasoning, automated experimental calibration and control for closed-loop discovery of quantum control sequences. Applied to a dense ensemble of spin-1 nitrogen-vacancy centers in diamond, QORA discovers AID-ROD, a series of AI-discovered sequences that nearly double the measured spin coherence time relative to the state-of-the-art human-designed sequence DROID-C3PO. Subsequence analysis by human researchers reveals a hierarchy of error suppression: 18-pulse blocks suppress linear and quadratic onsite disorder and common-mode drive-amplitude errors, while their arrangement over a longer cycle reduces dipolar interactions, providing a physical explanation for the observed improvement and robustness in AID-ROD. These results demonstrate AI-guided experimental discovery of robust quantum control and identify a design strategy for protecting coherence in interacting multi-level systems beyond qubits.

Abstract: As a consequence of the relativistic spectrum condition, quantum reference frames for translations on Minkowski space are never described by projection-valued measures, and in many cases fail a weaker spacetime localisation property known as the norm-$1$ condition. This has several implications for the local and observable structure of quantum field theories described relative to even the best available frame: for a large class of frame states, strict locality is replaced by almost locality, and microcausality by commutators that decay rapidly with increasing separation, corresponding to an effective smearing of the light cone by the localisation density of the frame state. Abstract: Eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) provides a central framework for understanding thermalization in isolated quantum systems. Abstract: Quantum Fisher information (QFI) is a fundamental quantum metrological quantity arising from the second order term in a Taylor expansion of a smooth divergence evaluated at nearby quantum states. Bosonic Gaussian states are common probe states for quantum metrological tasks and admit a complete characterization in terms of their first and second moments. In this work, we consider the quantum metrological task of estimating parameters that characterize a Gaussian unitary time evolution of a faithful bosonic Gaussian thermal state. As an example, we calculate entries of the $α$-$z$ information matrix for a two-mode Gaussian thermal state that has undergone a beam splitting operation. Abstract: In quantum dynamics, it is generally believed that interference effects lead to a sign problem for classical Monte Carlo sampling methods, rendering sampling ineffective. In this paper we show that this is generically not the case for the task of simulating the evolution of observables in random instances of circuit dynamics. Specifically, we consider two examples of observables which give large signals despite the randomness of the dynamics: first order out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) in unstructured random circuits without conservation laws and time-ordered charge-charge correlators (TOCs) in particle number-preserving random circuits. We observe numerically that the computational complexity of approximating TOCs and OTOCs scales polynomially in the circuit size and in the target precision. We study the accuracy of these algorithms by directly mapping their ensemble-averaged errors to partition functions of replica statistical models, allowing for perturbative analysis and numerics in $1$d brickwork geometries. Our theoretical analysis explains the empirical observation of polynomial complexity of these algorithms and is consistent with polynomial complexity extending to arbitrarily small precision and to the thermodynamic limit.

Abstract: Aggregate molecular systems are routinely understood by decomposing them into local structural units, such as monomers in J/H-aggregates, chemical moieties, motifs, and functional groups. Here, we ask how the symmetries of constituent structures relate to aggregate symmetry, and how this relation determines the constraints that global symmetry imposes on transitions between local symmetry sectors. We extract soft-output information from the lower-accuracy decoder efficiently for any QLDPC code, which allows us to identify decoding windows of low confidence. We simulate memory experiments of bivariate bicycle (BB) codes, using trapped-ion informed noise models. Depending on the physical error rate and BB code, we find up to 3-4 orders of magnitude logical error rate reduction compared to the $\texttt{BP-LSD}$ decoder, and up to 2-3 orders of magnitude lower logical error rate compared to the $\texttt{Beamsearch}$ decoder. By leveraging available classical processing time, our decoding protocol significantly boosts decoding accuracy, ultimately reducing the demands on increasing code size.

Abstract: The growing capabilities of quantum error-correction (QEC) experiments make it increasingly important to develop methods that predict or assess logical performance over many QEC cycles. Prior work has shown that a stabilizer QEC cycle subject to Pauli-stochastic noise can be modeled by a classical Markov process over syndrome states, with a logical Pauli channel attached to each syndrome transition. Here, we extend this framework to systems in which physical qubits are subject to leakage. We first develop the theory of leakage-dephased Pauli twirling and show that twirled processes can be expressed in terms of Pauli maps on the computational subspace, conditioned on transitions between leakage patterns. We apply this theory to noisy stabilizer QEC with leakage, demonstrating that a QEC cycle with leakage can be modeled by a classical Markov process over the joint syndrome and leakage patterns, with a logical Pauli channel attached to each joint transition. Finally, we derive a transfer-matrix description of repeated QEC cycles and an exact transfer-matrix expression for the success probability of a QEC memory experiment. This framework connects leaky error models of the individual components of the QEC cycle to many-cycle logical performance and provides a foundation for logical characterization and benchmarking methods that can account for leakage.

These results establish PB-VVPT as an accurate and computationally efficient approach to quantum information transfer in molecular polaritonic networks.

Furthermore, the lattice-surgery escape reaches a logical error rate per stabilizer round of about 0.14% with around 11% average shot retainment. These results establish lattice-surgery escape as a practical MSC protocol that benefits from a direct transition into a surface code, and demonstrate the lowest MSC error and the highest surface-code magic-state fidelity reported to date, advancing the experimental realization of fault-tolerant non-Clifford gadgets.

Abstract: We study synchronous and bisynchronous no-signalling correlations, defined over Cantor topological spaces, from the perspectives of both continuous variable quantum information and operator algebra theories. We focus on strategies, satisfying a natural no-signalling condition across the Cantor space levels, called graded, identifying a universal C*-algebra, whose tracial states of different types characterise synchronous correlations of respective types. The universal C*-algebra for bisynchronous graded correlations is shown to coincide with the C*-algebra of a projective limit of free wreath products of quantum permutation groups, introduced by Bhowmick, Goswami and Skalski, and a type description, analogous to the one in the synchronous case, is provided. Extending results in the finite case, we show that bisynchronous graded correlations canonically give rise to factorisable maps on type I factors, defined via Cantor permutation matrices. We examine non-local games over Cantor topological spaces, with inputs/outputs that are finite words of equal length, define corresponding game algebras, and characterise the perfect strategies of the games via the respective game algebras. In the case of the Cantor graph isomorphism game, we provide equivalent descriptions of the existence of perfect strategies of quantum commuting type, in terms of bigalois correspondences, factorisable maps, Cantor permutation intertwiners and game algebra non-triviality. Abstract: Electromagnetic simulations of device layouts to extract capacitance are computationally intensive, a major obstacle to accurate design of circuit devices. Machine learning models can bypass these simulations, but previous approaches have been restricted to layouts using only particular components, and require thousands of simulations of those same components as training data. We present QCap, a model that accurately predicts the Maxwell capacitance matrix of an arbitrary superconducting device layout in seconds, compared to minutes to hours of Ansys Q3D simulations. We develop a novel design-embedding algorithm that converts the layout into a universal graph format, allowing the model to work with any layout created with any tool. To achieve high accuracy, we obtain a coarse physics prior from a fast method of moments simulation of capacitance, then train the model to correct this prior. On 4,009 test designs withheld from training data the model outputs agree with Q3D simulations to a median error of 0.177%, and on never-seen component families it stays below 1% error. We demonstrate the model's scaling by obtaining the capacitance matrix of a 9-superconducting-qubit device in 12 seconds on a laptop (compared to 19.9 h and 228 GB of memory in Ansys) with sub-percent error, and capacitance of a (too large to simulate) 100-qubit, 781-conductor device in 3.4 min on a laptop. We provide the open-source models and data on SQuADDS and Hugging Face. Our results provide a powerful tool for large-scale accurate circuit design and guide the development of models to bypass other physical simulations.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可训练性（landscape可优化）不能保证在固定深度与评估预算下到达哈密顿量选定的目标态；作者欲针对这一"操作层面失效"给出可操作的修补手段，而非仅停留在可训练性分析。

Method: 构造受限的局部Pauli旋转池：由低阶宿主—哈密顿量对易子以及固定同支撑（same-support）伴随轴共同定义；从中选取一个紧凑的补全层，随后进行完整重优化。设置两组预先声明的选择器，并与逐试验的非可部署控制包络作对照。

Result: 189组六比特配对试验中，未修补线路严格成功0/189；非可部署的逐试验控制包络109/189；两个预声明选择器分别为133/189与127/189。修补在横场Ising上劣于匹配控制，在纯ZZ MaxCut上效果弱，在加权MIS上受规模限制。在固定深度几何局域性下，修补后的损失与梯度可经典计算，相应基态目标在测试规模内可由低键维张量网络近似。

Conclusion: 修补方法揭示的是"可训练性—到达性—经典可及性"三重权衡，而非量子优势；对易子引导的补全层是缓解固定深度下到达失败的有效但非普适手段。

Abstract: Trainability does not guarantee low-energy arrival: a shallow quantum circuit can be optimizable yet fail, under fixed depth and evaluation budgets, to reach Hamiltonian-selected states. We introduce a commutator-guided repair for this operational failure mode. Low-order host--Hamiltonian commutators and fixed same-support companion axes define a restricted pool of local Pauli rotations, from which a compact completion layer is selected before full reoptimization. Across 189 paired six-qubit trials, unrepaired circuits achieve 0/189 strict successes, a non-deployable per-trial control envelope achieves 109/189, and two predeclared selectors achieve 133/189 and 127/189. The method reveals a trainability--arrival--classical-accessibility tradeoff rather than quantum advantage.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于稳定子的子空间方法能大幅减少电子结构计算的量子资源，但所得投影忽略了哈密顿量与保留子空间之外组态之间的耦合，导致能量误差。

Method: 以CS-VQE为代表应用，将稳定子投影消除的Pauli项作为微扰算符，在优化后的参考态上生成外部流形；用Epstein-Nesbet型二阶修正或保留外部空间内耦合的resolvent(分辨算符)形式计算能量贡献；利用稳定子约束参考态的因子化结构，在缩减的contextual寄存器上通过Pauli期望值求矩阵元，无需显式重构全空间波函数。

Abstract: Stabilizer-based subspace methods can substantially reduce the quantum resources required for electronic structure calculations, but the resulting projection neglects Hamiltonian couplings to configurations outside the retained subspace. In this work, we introduce Stabilizer-Subspace Perturbation Theory (SSPT) to recover the associated correlation contributions, using the Contextual Subspace Variational Quantum Eigensolver (CS-VQE) as a representative application. The central idea is to exploit the Pauli terms eliminated by stabilizer projection as perturbative operators that generate an external manifold from the optimized reference state. Their energetic contributions are evaluated using an Epstein-Nesbet-type second-order correction or a resolvent-based formulation that retains couplings within the external space. By exploiting the factorized structure of the stabilizer-constrained reference state, the required matrix elements can be evaluated through Pauli expectation values on the reduced contextual register without explicitly reconstructing the full-space wave function. Molecular benchmarks demonstrate substantial reductions in projection-induced energy errors, allowing chemical precision to be achieved with fewer contextual qubits in several cases. These results establish stabilizer-guided perturbation theory as a promising strategy for improving the accuracy of resource-efficient quantum chemistry calculations.

</details>

Exact contributions provide offline supervision without being required at deployment. The results reveal both promise and a clear limitation: LLMs can identify useful measurement groups, yet without guidance, local models do not consistently prioritize the physical information that matters most. Our learned controller uses these cues more effectively. Abstract: Exceptional-point sensors exploit singular non-Hermitian dynamics to enhance their response to weak perturbations. Abstract: Which quantum causal structures can account for observed spatiotemporal correlations without access to an intervention-complete description of the underlying process? In particular, can such correlations distinguish a temporal chain, a common cause, and a common effect? Here, we address these questions using three-qubit pseudo-density operators constructed from experimentally accessible Pauli correlations. We derive necessary and sufficient conditions for pseudo-Markov compatibility with the chain, fork, and collider structures by relaxing complete positivity while preserving the corresponding quantum Markov constraints. Despite their distinct causal mechanisms, all three pseudo-Markov compatibility problems reduce to linear feasibility tests, without requiring full-rank marginal states. We further establish a unified semidefinite-programming approach to physical realizability and quantify the minimum violation of complete positivity through Choi negativity. Using a two-parameter family of pseudo-density operators, we demonstrate that different causal structures admit overlapping yet non-nested sets of correlations. Our results show that quantum causal explanations need not be unique, but experimentally accessible correlations can nevertheless distinguish and exclude candidate causal structures.

Abstract: Superconducting quantum computing faces a critical scalability bottleneck arising from the escalating complexity of traditional analog control infrastructure. Single-Flux-Quantum(SFQ) systems operate at cryogenic temperatures and offer a potential route to addressing these challenges.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强周期调制不仅改造量子比特间相互作用，还会把耗散通道重组为 Floquet 通道梳，因此需要厘清调制深度、对称性与耗散对纠缠稳态的联合影响，并评估主方程构建方式的可靠性。

Result: 基态 concurrence 从 0.45 升至 >0.8，但稳态 concurrence 除对称选定的 Floquet 共振外接近无驱动值；共振处出现纠缠死亡，在一定调制深度范围内驱动周期内任意时刻纠缠均为零。所有耗散通道连接两扇区，故仅偶数扇区反交叉可致纠缠死亡，条件是能隙超过阻尼率；相邻奇数交叉不改变 concurrence。耗散子构建基的选择可使纠缠改变达 0.2（Ohmic 库）与 0.5（平谱库）；secular 近似仅在能隙与阻尼相当的反交叉附近有影响；低频谱权重时基的选择最关键。

Conclusion: 纠缠工程不能仅看周期平均哈密顿量；稳态纠缠受 Floquet 通道结构与宇称/交换对称性支配。破坏宇称的横向场可解除选择定则，恢复共振处纠缠。

Abstract: Periodic modulation of a qubit--qubit coupling is a standard way to engineer quantum interactions, but a strong modulation also reorganizes the transitions through which the qubits dissipate into a comb of Floquet channels. We study two identical, strongly coupled qubits whose transverse coupling is modulated anharmonically, as by a nonlinear coupler, with each qubit damped by its own reservoir. The modulation raises the ground-state concurrence of the cycle-averaged Hamiltonian from 0.45 to above 0.8, yet the steady-state concurrence stays close to its undriven value except at Floquet resonances selected by symmetry. There it undergoes entanglement death, vanishing at all times in the drive cycle over a finite range of modulation depths. Parity and exchange symmetry split the Floquet spectrum into an even sector, whose branches anticross at multiphoton resonances, and an exactly solvable odd sector, whose branches cross. Every dissipative channel connects the two sectors, so only the even anticrossings cause entanglement death, provided their gap exceeds the damping rate, whereas the nearby odd crossings leave the concurrence unchanged. Comparing time-independent and Floquet master equations, with and without the secular approximation, we show that the basis in which the dissipator is built changes the entanglement by up to 0.2 for an Ohmic bath and 0.5 for a flat one, whereas the secular approximation matters only near anticrossings with gaps comparable to the damping. The basis matters most when the bath has spectral weight at low frequency, where the drive moves dissipative channels through zero frequency. A transverse field that breaks parity lifts the selection rule.

Abstract: We investigate the emergence and persistence of maximal entanglement between a two-level atom and a single-mode quantized electromagnetic field inside a cavity, whose dynamics are governed by the Jaynes-Cummings Hamiltonian. By considering the atom initially in its excited state, we further demonstrate that the persistence of maximal atom-field entanglement strongly depends on the initial state of the field. For field states whose photon-number distributions have no two consecutive occupied photon-number levels, the local atomic coherence remains zero throughout the evolution. Consequently, complete collapse of the atomic population inversion for an extended period leads to persistent maximal atom-field entanglement. In contrast, for other field states, the interaction generates local atomic coherence, resulting in transient maximal entanglement. Our results demonstrate how the choice of the initial field state controls the temporal characteristics of entanglement in the Jaynes-Cummings system. The generation and persistence of maximal atom-field entanglement in such systems could be valuable for applications in quantum information processing.

### [57] [CHEQ: A Concurrent Hardware Evaluation Platform for QEC Decoders via Batch-Invariant Circuit-Level Noise Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22247)
*Sechan Park,Kyeongwon Lee,Mundo Jung,Chaebin Jung,Woojoo Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: CHEQ：集成片上采样、并发解码与评分的FPGA量子纠错验证平台，以几何跳过实现高效触发采样，在单芯片上完成10^10次采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件QEC解码器部署前需验证逻辑错误抑制与时序合规性；稀有逻辑失败与时限违规需要海量采样，而算法级软件评估无法直接测得硬件响应时间。电路级噪声模型误差机制多、低物理错误率下每次射击触发少，逐射击穷举扫描效率低。

Conclusion: 片上几何跳过采样可高保真、低开销地替代主机端射击生成，使硬件QEC解码器的大规模统计验证与时序合规评估在单FPGA上高效完成。

Abstract: Hardware quantum error correction (QEC) decoders require validation of logical error suppression and timing compliance before deployment. Rare logical failures and deadline violations demand extensive sampling, while algorithm-level software evaluation does not directly measure hardware response times. Circuit-level noise models contain many error mechanisms but few firings per shot at low physical error rates, making exhaustive per-shot scanning inefficient. We present CHEQ, an FPGA platform integrating on-chip sampling, concurrent decoder evaluation, scoring, and RISC-V control. Its Sampling Units use geometric skipping to sample firing locations from detector error models and amortize mechanism traversal across shots within each batch. Under independent random draws and exact geometric sampling, the shot distribution is invariant to batch size, enabling a memory-throughput tradeoff. A throughput model guides joint selection of the Sampling Unit count, batch size, and decoder count under FPGA resource constraints. Across evaluated conditions, the pooled one-sided 95% upper bound on the weighted root-mean-square relative firing-rate difference from Stim is 0.16%. Abstract: Realistic quantum hardware often exhibits biased noise, but at the circuit level this bias may affect only a subset of operations and need not be transferred uniformly to logical errors. We first construct geometry-dependent logical error rate models from unbiased circuit-level simulations. We then use logical sensitivities to compare the responses to localized idle-bias and global physical error perturbations, defining logical-error-type-dependent response coefficients from an unbiased reference circuit without fitting to finite-bias data. Geometries selected by the model are validated through direct biased circuit-level simulations. At a physical error rate of 0.004 and a target logical error rate of 10^{-3}, the validated rectangular codes reduce physical qubit overhead by up to 42.4% and 26.2% under the two noise models, respectively, relative to unrotated square codes. These results provide a practical link between circuit-level bias response and resource-efficient surface code design.

*Xusheng Xu,Runqing Zhang,Hongtao Xue,Jize Han,Jingwei Wen,Ruoyu Zhou,Lei Jin,Xiaoxiao Hu,Gui-Lu Long*

TL;DR: quspect 是一款开源量子计算 SDK，本文描述并评估其电路编译器：以"路由—合成交互"为设计核心，最高优化级别下对筛选后的路由候选做耦合约束重合成并按代价择优，结合 A* 最小化 CX 数。在 55 个电路、5 种拓扑上，两比特门数为 Qiskit O3 的 0.616 倍、深度 0.592 倍。

Conclusion: 将路由与耦合约束下的重合成统一在同一优化流程中、以合成后代价筛选路由候选，可显著优于主流编译器的门数与深度表现；A* 在预算内到达目标时可证明 CX 数最小；消融实验明确了集成流水线中各组件各自的贡献。

Abstract: quspect is an open-source quantum computing SDK that provides circuit construction, C++ state-vector simulation with adjoint gradients, support for variational and combinatorial-optimization algorithms, Hamiltonian simulation, control-pulse envelopes and backends for several cloud quantum processors. Here we describe and evaluate its circuit compiler. Routing and synthesis interact: inserting a SWAP can change the circuit structure that later optimization exploits. At its highest optimization level, the compiler evaluates a screened set of routing candidates by their cost after coupling-constrained resynthesis. The pipeline combines parity-state A* search for small CNOT-phase regions, heuristic synthesis for larger regions, and numerical three-qubit instantiation with a per-block 10^-9 check. For a fixed phase polynomial, A* certifies the minimum CX count when it reaches a goal within budget. On an evaluation set of 55 circuits from 41 arXiv papers on five topologies, the compiler uses 0.616 times as many two-qubit gates as Qiskit O3 (95% circuit-cluster bootstrap interval 0.555-0.679) at 0.592 times its depth. BQSKit level 2 reaches 0.853 and tket 0.930. On a 17-qubit superconducting processor, the compiled circuits had the fewest CZ gates in all six scenarios of the main run. </details>

Abstract: In quantum optimal control, gradient methods are often used to optimize functions of a state or propagator at a final time, while experimentally addressable observables and performance metrics may depend on multi-time correlations. The method incorporates the time evolution of an auxiliary operator in QRT into the forward propagation and backward adjoint propagation used in GRAPE. As an application, we consider the trade-off between the photon-emission probability and the conditional Bell-state fidelity in heralded remote entanglement generation among two nonidentical atom--cavity systems. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子脉冲占据一定频率范围，仅在载频处抑制反射并不能最小化整个透射脉冲的误差，需要在有限带宽内重新分配反射零点。

Abstract: A photon pulse occupies a range of frequencies, so suppressing reflection at its carrier need not minimize the error of the transmitted pulse. We study two separated emitters coupled to a common waveguide at several contact points. Interference between the contact paths can make the coupling that drives each emitter quadratic in frequency detuning. With matched propagation and emitter resonances centered on the carrier, the reflected fields cancel once more. Their combined reflection probability is then sixth order at zero loss. Separating each local double zero into two simple zeros can nevertheless improve transmission of a finite pulse. We minimize the leading narrowband error within this common static adjustment, counting reflection, absorption, and distortion of the transmitted mode. For a Gaussian pulse without loss, the minimum is two-fifths of the error for coincident zeros. Independent emitter loss favors a smaller separation because photons entering separate reservoirs do not share the cancellation of the reflected fields. The optimum follows from the pulse spectrum and the relative weight of these two processes. Uniform and Su--Schrieffer--Heeger waveguides realize the mechanism. In the latter, coordinated contact and resonance adjustments also preserve the full ratio of complex transmission amplitudes for opposite incidence directions. Abstract: Recent advances in artificial intelligence and quantum technologies have driven the emergence of quantum machine learning, yet imperfections in near-term quantum devices remain a major obstacle for achieving practical quantum advantage. Abstract: Encoded fusion combines probabilistic physical fusions to perform a logical Bell measurement. The lower bound applies to arbitrary perfect strategies that choose batches and bases using previous outcomes. We also characterize the batch sequences that can occur on an all-failure branch of a perfect strategy by a necessary and sufficient graph adjacency condition. Abstract: We propose a method towards demonstrating a quantum superposition of a massive mechanical resonator without the need for exact ground-state cooling, a requirement that becomes increasingly difficult to satisfy for low-frequency resonators. Interference between the resonator states can be observed when a series of $\tfracπ{2}$-pulses is applied to the spin-qubit within the coherence time of the resonator. The protocol describes a true which-path interference experiment suitable for use at ultra-low temperatures, since it requires only three $\tfracπ{2}$-pulses, separated in time by half a mechanical period, thus minimizing the heat-load and duration of the experiment. By analysing the experimental requirements necessary to successfully execute the protocol, we show that the protocol is suitable for resonators in a broad regime of resonance frequencies, ranging from 1kHz resonators, which have the advantage of a relatively high spin-coupling, to resonators of more than 100kHz, whose mechanical mode is easier to cool. Abstract: We present an open-system quantum theory of forward superradiant bursts in extended two-level atomic ensembles using coupled Heisenberg-Langevin and Maxwell propagation equations. Starting from a fully inverted state with zero mean polarization, local dipole correlations initiate emission without a prescribed tipping angle or stochastic seed. Abstract: Exhibiting the superiority of energy storage and conversion over its classical counterpart, quantum battery (QB) emerges as a promising platform to revolutionize traditional energy technologies. Here, we propose a decoherence-tolerant QB consisting of a continuous-variable bosonic mode. Acting as the quantum resource, the utilized squeezing endows the QB with unprecedented energy capacity and ergotropy. Through investigating the non-Markovian decoherence effect on the QB, we find that such squeezing enhanced performance can be persistently preserved under the influence of decoherence as long as a bound state is present in the energy spectrum of the total system formed by the QB and the environment. We find exactly when each departs from separability and that outer separability keeps the operational content of separability for the available measurements: outer separable states, entangled or not, admit local hidden variable models for every behaviour these measurements generate and, when the steered party is unrestricted, local hidden state models for every assemblage the steering party's measurements generate. Such hidden state models do not imply outer separability in general, but we identify conditions under which they do.

Abstract: We investigate the quantum optimal control of an antiferromagnetic XXZ spin chain with open boundary conditions, driven solely by a local time-dependent magnetic field applied to a single terminal spin. By optimizing the control field via Krotov's algorithm, we evaluate the feasibility of preparing multi-particle many-body states, including highly polarized Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ)-type states and study the mechanisms by which the control is applied. We demonstrate that global spin-flip symmetry restricts reachable state spaces, strictly confining dynamics within the parity sector of the initial ground state and requiring parity-adapted linear combinations to avoid fundamental population upper bounds. Beyond symmetry constraints, driven population transfer does not occur via direct multi-spin flips, but proceeds stepwise across intermediate magnetization sectors ($|S_{\mathrm{tot}}^z| = 0 \to 1 \to 2 \to 3$) mediated by resonant frequency components matching the internal energy gaps. To overcome optimization stagnation caused by the small overlap mechanism in gradient updates, we introduce a four-stage sequential target protocol and compared it to spectrally filtered, time-localized frequency seed pulses. Combining frequency-tailored seed fields with an extended propagation time window yields GHZ state preparation fidelities exceeding $0.927$ establishing that a single local actuator is sufficient for high-fidelity multipartite state engineering in interacting spin networks.

</details>

Abstract: The spectrum of artificial atoms coupled to resonators in the ultrastrong coupling regime have been analyzed using the quantum Rabi model over a wide range of coupling strengths. However, the accuracy of models obtained by truncating multilevel artificial atoms to two levels, as well as the correspondence between effective parameters extracted from spectral fitting and the bare parameters of the circuit Hamiltonian, depends on the multilevel structure of the system. In this study, I analyze the spectrum of an ultrastrongly coupled circuit consisting of a multilevel superconducting artificial atom and a resonator using a circuit Hamiltonian, and investigate the effects of truncating higher atomic levels on the low-energy spectrum. Based on Löwdin partitioning, I evaluate the contributions of virtual transitions through higher atomic levels and show that diagonal energy corrections reduce the errors arising from simple two-level truncation over a wide parameter range. I also show that there are parameter regimes in which readjustment of the effective Rabi parameters successfully reproduces the multilevel spectrum, while systematic deviations remain in other regimes. These results demonstrate the necessity of separately evaluating two-level truncation, perturbative corrections, and effective-parameter fitting in ultrastrongly coupled systems, and provide guidance for quantitative spectral analysis of multilevel quantum circuits.

Abstract: The local distinguishability of a set of orthogonal quantum states can be used to reduce the amount of quantum state transmission in quantum protocols and lower system operational costs and quantum resource overhead. We classify all feasible sets of five six-partite OPSs into five categories with 249 cases in total, and prove that these sets can be perfectly distinguished by local operations and classical communication, except for two cases. For five six-partite OPSs corresponding to these two exceptional cases, we further discuss their local distinguishability. Our work fully characterizes the local distinguishability of all feasible sets of five six-partite OPSs.

### [71] [Entanglement Lower Bounds for Symmetric Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22621)
*Davi Castro-Silva,Ryan Dancy,Sergii Strelchuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将经典对称电路与描述复杂性的联系推广到量子模型，证明电路规模与纠缠宽度之间存在权衡，得到图性质的可判定性下界，并刻画了无纠缠限制与极小纠缠宽度下的计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典对称电路通过描述复杂性可获得强无条件下界，作者希望把这一思路推广到 Castro-Silva、Gur 与 Strelchuk（ITCS 2026）定义的量子对称计算模型，从而在量子电路上获得类似的规模–纠缠权衡下界。

Method: 在量子对称电路模型下研究电路规模与纠缠宽度（每层状态在计算基输入下可分解为至多 b 个量子比特的子态时的最小 b）的权衡；提出基于偏迹（partial trace）的新量子卵石游戏（quantum pebble game），由此建立不可区分性定理，将经典卵石游戏下界迁移到纠缠受限的量子电路。

Result: 证明存在一个经典多项式时间可判定的图性质，任何在 n 顶点图上、纠缠宽度为 o(n/log n)、规模多项式的对称量子电路族都无法计算它；该下界适用于任意单比特与两比特门、深度不限，且在仅对偶置换对称的较弱定义下仍成立。反方向上：无纠缠限制时，BQP 中每个图性质都可由一致多项式规模对称量子电路计算；纠缠宽度为 1 时，可计算带计数的定点逻辑（FPC）可定义的所有图性质；并给出 MAX 3XOR 上 QAOA 的高效对称实现。

Conclusion: 对称量子电路在纠缠宽度受限时存在本质性的规模下界，而与经典描述复杂性结果形成对应；无限制或极小纠缠宽度下其能力分别覆盖 BQP 图性质与 FPC 可定义性质，量子卵石游戏为量子优化算法的纠缠下界研究提供了工具与起点。

Abstract: Symmetric circuits require every symmetry of the input to extend to an automorphism of the circuit. In the classical setting, their connection to descriptive complexity yields strong unconditional lower bounds. We extend this approach to the quantum model of symmetric computation defined by Castro-Silva, Gur, and Strelchuk (ITCS 2026), proving a tradeoff between circuit size and entanglement width (the smallest $b$ such that, on every computational-basis input, the state after each layer factors into states on at most $b$ qubits each).
  Our main result identifies a graph property decidable in classical polynomial time that no polynomial-size family of symmetric quantum circuits on $n$-vertex graphs with entanglement width $o(n/\log n)$ can compute. The bound holds for circuits with arbitrary one-qubit and two-qubit gates and unrestricted depth, and remains valid under the weaker notion of symmetry with respect to even permutations of vertices. Our proof is based on a new quantum pebble game based on the partial trace. The resulting indistinguishability theorem transfers classical pebble-game lower bounds to quantum circuits with restricted entanglement.
  By contrast, without entanglement restrictions, we show that every graph property in BQP can be computed by a uniform family of polynomial-size symmetric quantum circuits. Restricting to circuits with no entanglement at all, we show that polynomial-size symmetric quantum circuits with entanglement width 1 can compute every graph property definable in fixed-point logic with counting. Finally, we give an efficient symmetric implementation of QAOA for MAX 3XOR, providing a starting point for studying entanglement lower bounds in quantum optimization algorithms.

</details>


### [72] [Quantum speed limits from symmetries: A numerical analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22630)
*Santana Lujan,Marco Wiedmann,Daniel Burgarth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将目标特化量子速度极限（QSL）的构造转化为凸半定规划，并配合对称性空间计算与非凸搜索，给出可复现的数值流程，在CNOT、SWAP与Dicke态制备上改进已有对称性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有从控制系统动力学李代数对称性及"对称性恢复微扰"直接导出目标特化QSL的理论工作，无需解运动方程；但实际中寻找合适的对称性与恢复微扰高度依赖物理直觉，且无法保证界限的紧致性。

Method: 对给定对称性，将最小恢复微扰表述为凸半定规划（SDP），可全局最优求解，从而得到该对称性下最紧的速度极限；SDP作为数值方法的一个模块，另配可容许对称性空间的显式计算，以及在该空间上的搜索——因搜索非凸，采用梯度优化、黑箱启发式或注入物理动机的候选对称性。

Result: 应用于三个算例：两比特CNOT门、无自旋粒子链上的SWAP、量子比特环上的Dicke态制备。结果优于已知对称性界限，SWAP尤为显著——对已知解析对称性求解SDP使界限收紧约2.5倍。Dicke态制备的界限随系统规模增大而下降，归因于与对称性无关的微扰上限。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了直接从系统哈密顿量与目标操作构建目标特化QSL的可复现数值程序，并揭示了对称性无关微扰上限对界限标度行为的限制。

Abstract: Target-specific quantum speed limits (QSLs) bound the minimal time required to realize a given operation. Recent theoretical work derives these limits directly from the symmetries of the dynamical Lie algebra of the control system and a symmetry-restoring perturbation, bypassing the need to solve the equations of motion. In practice, however, identifying a suitable symmetry and a perturbation that restores it demands strong physical intuition and offers no guarantee of tightness. We show that, for any given symmetry, the minimal restoring perturbation is the solution of a convex semidefinite program, which can be solved to global optimality and thus yields the tightest achievable speed limit for that symmetry. This semidefinite program is one building block of our numerical method, alongside an explicit computation of the space of admissible symmetries and a search over this space. Since this search is non-convex, it is addressed with gradient-based optimization, black-box heuristics, or the injection of physically motivated candidate symmetries. We apply the method to three examples: a two-qubit CNOT gate, a SWAP on a spinless-particle chain, and a Dicke state preparation on a qubit ring. Our results improve on previously known symmetry-based bounds, most notably for the SWAP, where solving the semidefinite program for the known analytical symmetry tightens the bound by a factor of approximately 2.5. For the Dicke state preparation, however, the bounds decrease with system size, which we trace back to a symmetry-independent perturbation ceiling. This work provides a reproducible numerical procedure for constructing target-specific quantum speed limits directly from the system Hamiltonians and the target operation.

</details>

Conclusion: 该方案为利用连续变量测量资源进行条件性Bloch球态调控提供了统一分析框架，明确了态制备任务与通用量子门的区别，并为参数估计与实验实现提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate measurement-conditioned state steering of a qubit coupled to a continuous-variable pointer through a state-dependent displacement interaction. A position measurement on the pointer induces a nonunitary conditional transformation of the qubit, allowing its post-measurement state to move along a trajectory on the Bloch sphere. We derive the conditional state, the measurement-outcome distribution, and the corresponding Bloch-sphere coordinates. For a real Gaussian pointer, the conditional state follows a meridian, whereas a phase-engineered Gaussian pointer produces a trajectory with a measurement-dependent azimuthal angle. We further describe outcome-dependent unitary feedback for preparing a prescribed target state from a known initial qubit state. We distinguish this state-preparation task from implementing a universal deterministic single-qubit gate, which does not follow from the proposed interaction and measurement alone. Finally, we derive the quantum Fisher information of the joint qubit--pointer state for estimating the displacement-generating parameter and discuss the implications of pointer squeezing, phase engineering, and experimental implementation. The results provide a framework for analysing conditional Bloch-sphere steering using continuous-variable measurement resources.

*Daniel Maroto Sanchez,Christian Velasco-Gallego,Miriam Bastante Chichon,Jaime Álvarez-Benayas,Tsoulakos Nikolaos,J. A. Bravo-Montes*

Abstract: The Internet of Ships enables vessels to generate vast amounts of data, yet missing values remain a persistent issue. To the best of the authors' knowledge, existing imputation techniques in this domain rely exclusively on classical machine learning. This study investigates quantum support vector regression (QSVR) for imputing missing values in ship performance data. ZZFeatureMap_1 achieved the highest average R2 (0.991 in the performance comparison conducted with 60% of the dataset, 90% missing data, and 14 input features, for instance) and the lowest aggregate imputation errors, while the radial basis function kernel was the strongest classical baseline, delivering highly competitive results.

Abstract: The Quantum Rabi Model (QRM), consisting of a single qubit coupled to a harmonic oscillator, provides a paradigmatic platform for exploring critical phenomena investigated for their applications in quantum technologies. In the superradiant phase of the QRM with coupling strength above the critical point, the bosonic ground state exhibits temporally stable quantum non-Gaussian features. However, the quantum non-Gaussian features of such superradiant ground states can be fragile against loss during preparation. Here, we show that persistent quantum non-Gaussian features can instead be realized below the critical point through weak periodic qubit modulation. We analyze an adiabatic ramp protocol in which the modulation amplitude is gradually increased, allowing the system to approximately follow the instantaneous Floquet state. When the modulation period is tuned close to Poincaré-Birkhoff resonances, the resulting state exhibits pronounced quantum non-Gaussian features in the directly measurable Wigner function with a complex phase-space structure that is considerably more robust against loss than that of the superradiant ground state. Abstract: Quantum dynamics can encode interference information that conventional correlation functions leave unresolved. Here we show that the imaginary component of the extended Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability reveals dynamical distinctions absent from out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) formed from two repeated Hermitian observables at infinite temperature. We interpret its real and imaginary components as complementary quadratures of interference between many-body echo amplitudes. We prove that the imaginary component vanishes for every unitary evolution if either projective measurement has two outcomes, whereas nonreal values arise for generic unitary dynamics when it has at least three (e.g., spin-1 systems). We develop an algorithm to reconstruct the full quasiprobability from covariances of local probe signals over repeated preparations of randomised initial states, using forward evolution and probe readout only. In a spin-1 chain, a nonzero imaginary signal distinguishes a real Hamiltonian $H$ from $-H$ despite identical corresponding OTOCs, and identifies opposite sides of a pair-conversion resonance at short times while dipolar and quadrupolar OTOCs remain nearly unchanged. These results show how temporal interference resolves dynamical ambiguities and provides additional constraints for Hamiltonian inference.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum random number generators have become a cornerstone technology for modern digital cryptography, including key generation, quantum key distribution, and post-quantum cryptographic systems. While existing reviews typically concentrate on specific entropy sources or implementation platforms, a comprehensive perspective encompassing the complete QRNG pipeline remains largely absent. This work addresses this gap by providing a unified review of the theoretical foundations, practical methodologies, and technological developments that underpin modern QRNGs. We examine randomness from both classical and quantum perspectives and survey the principal discrete-variable and continuous-variable entropy sources, together with entropy estimation, randomness certification, extraction techniques, randomness enhancement protocols, and statistical validation methods. Beyond the fundamental components, we review representative implementations reported in the literature, comparing performance metrics, architectural trade-offs, integration strategies, and attainable generation rates across different technological platforms. Practical deployment challenges, including scalability, trust assumptions, hardware integration, and cryptographic adoption, are also discussed. By complementing the discussion with a broad survey of state-of-the-art implementations and applications, this review provides researchers with a comprehensive reference on the current landscape, open challenges, and future directions of quantum random number generation.

Abstract: Low autocorrelation binary sequences (LABS) are the phase codes of pulse compression radar and the synchronization words of digital communication, and finding the sequence of a given length whose aperiodic autocorrelations have the smallest sum of squares is a hard binary optimization problem. The quantum optimization methods tested on LABS so far return good sequences, but they cannot show that a sequence is optimal or bound how far from optimal it may be. We show that a quantum optimization algorithm can deliver proven optimality. In our quantum-classical branch and bound, fixed-angle QAOA samples the Hamiltonians of the nodes of the search tree, its samples supply candidate sequences and the branching choice, and classical bounds prune the tree, so that the algorithm returns either a proof of optimality or, if it is stopped early, the best sequence found with a certified lower bound and gap. It works on the native quartic Hamiltonian without penalty terms, and its correctness does not depend on the sampler. With QAOA at depth p= 12 and at p= 1 and 1,000 shots per node, it proved the optima of all lengths from 2 to 30, where from length 27 on the root node is split without being sampled. Every run records both bounds against time, and its primal-dual integral measures its convergence. We claim no speedup.

</details>

Abstract: Memory effects in continuous-variable (CV) photonic systems can make standard Markovian Gaussian noise models inaccurate, even when the average noise strength is correctly calibrated. We propose a theoretical and simulation-based tomography framework for reconstructing such memory effects directly from time-resolved homodyne data. The method estimates an effective time-local Gaussian generator, represented by drift and diffusion matrices, using a regularized maximumlikelihood procedure constrained by complete positivity (CP) of the finite-time Gaussian channel. This description covers any Gaussian-preserving noise dynamics for which a well-defined time-local generator exists, rather than being restricted to particular noise mechanisms. Non-Markovianity is then diagnosed at the generator level through temporary violations of local CP-divisibility, rather than from time dependence alone. This distinction is important because ordinary nonstationary noise can remain CP-divisible, whereas genuine memory can produce a non-CP-divisible reduced generator while the accumulated channel remains physical. Numerical benchmarks covering memorydependent loss, nonstationary thermal excitation, and pseudomode-mediated memory show that the reconstruction distinguishes CP-divisible time-dependent noise from genuine non-CP-divisible dynamics and captures the corresponding signatures in the Gaussian covariance evolution. The framework therefore provides a practical route for diagnosing non-Markovian Gaussian noise in CV photonic quantum systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 态转换是量子资源理论的核心问题，而催化（不消耗辅助态）在纠缠与相干理论中已有丰富结论；虚数性（imaginarity）作为新兴资源，其催化转换的能力边界与催化剂角色尚不明确，且与纠缠/相干理论可能存在本质差异，值得刻画。

Method: 在虚数性资源理论框架下（自由操作为实操作），分别考察确定性催化转换与随机-近似催化转换；对后者按"失败时是否要求催化剂精确恢复""是否允许残余虚数性复用"分类讨论，推导最优成功概率及单次转换成功率与残余虚数性之间的权衡关系。

Result: (1) 确定性催化转换中，最大虚数纯态是唯一催化剂——与纠缠、相干理论中最大纠缠/最大相干态无法实现确定性催化转换形成对比；(2) 随机-近似且失败时不要求恢复催化剂时，任意含虚数性的纯态均可作催化剂，并给出最优成功概率；(3) 若无论成败均要求精确恢复催化剂，最优成功概率与无催化时相同，催化无增益；(4) 放宽失败时精确恢复要求，得到单次转换成功概率与催化剂残余虚数性的权衡关系；(5) 初始态可重新制备且残余虚数性可在失败后复用时，N（N≥2）轮催化转换可进一步提升成功率，并给出最大总体成功概率。

Conclusion: 虚数性资源理论中的催化行为与纠缠、相干理论显著不同：最大虚数纯态可充当确定性催化转换的唯一通用催化剂。催化剂的价值取决于恢复约束的严格程度——精确恢复抵消催化优势，而允许残余虚数性保留与复用则可在多轮中累积提升成功概率，体现了催化剂的"资源暂存"作用。

Abstract: State conversion is a central problem in quantum resource theories. In this work, we investigate catalytic state transformations within the resource theory of imaginarity. Catalytic imaginarity transformations enable otherwise impossible state transformations using only real operations, assisted by an auxiliary system with imaginarity that is not consumed. For deterministic catalytic conversion, we show that a maximally imaginary pure state is the unique catalyst. This result contrasts with the resource theories of entanglement and coherence, where maximally entangled and maximally coherent catalysts cannot enable such deterministic conversions under their respective free operations. For stochastic-approximate catalytic conversion, if we do not require catalyst recovery upon failure, any pure state with imaginarity can serve as a catalyst, and we derive the optimal success probability. If the catalyst is required to be recovered exactly regardless of whether the catalytic state transformation succeeds or fails, the optimal success probability coincides with that without a catalyst, and thus no advantage arises from catalysis. If exact catalyst recovery upon failure is relaxed, we find a tradeoff relation between the success probability of a single conversion and the catalyst's residual imaginarity after transformation. When the initial state is re-prepared and the catalyst's residual imaginarity can be reused after failure, the success probability can be further improved over $N$ ($N \geq 2$) rounds of catalytic conversion, and we obtain the corresponding maximum overall success probability.

</details>

Motivation: 被动接收端基选择（passive BB84）使用阈值探测器时，探测基的分布会依赖入射光子数，使其与基选择由独立随机性决定的主动BB84之间的关系并不显然，缺少直接的密钥率分析方法。

Conclusion: 模块化归约允许把公共参考协议的证明复用于不同实现（主动/被动基选择），避免为每种被动实现重复构造安全证明。

Abstract: In BB84 with passive receiver basis choice (passive BB84) and threshold detectors, the distribution of detection bases can depend on the incoming photon number. Its relation to BB84 with active receiver basis choice (active BB84), in which the basis is chosen using independent randomness, is therefore nontrivial. We establish a reduction theorem that expresses the secret-key-length formula for passive BB84 in terms of an active BB84 formula. The theorem allows a passive key-length formula to be obtained by simple substitutions into an existing active formula, without constructing a passive-specific security proof from scratch. It applies to key-length formulas derived using the Shor--Preskill, Koashi, and Renner approaches, because the reduction uses only properties shared by these approaches. Our result illustrates a modular approach that separates receiver analysis from the security proof and allows the proof of a common reference protocol to be reused for different implementations.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出DB-MEX：将双括号量子算法与系综方法结合，用单个递归编译电路将p+1个正交态演化为低能本征态。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化学、多体物理与优化中的许多问题需要哈密顿量的激发态；双括号算法无需参数化拟设即可递归编译电路，但此前主要针对对角化、基态与热态制备，尚未扩展到激发态。

Method: 将系综方法与双括号流结合，同时演化p+1个正交归一态，使其按构造保持正交性。电路近似的是演化态投影算子加权和的双括号流，态数与权重为设计参数：态数决定目标最高激发态，权重决定是制备最低p+1个本征态、仅第p激发态，还是仅其张成空间；对后者用后处理从张成空间恢复各本征态。

Result: 证明双括号流及其离散化（小步长下）对几乎所有初态收敛到目标，并给出由权重与能隙乘积构成的显式局部收敛速率。

Conclusion: DB-MEX以单一递归电路统一处理多激发态问题，权重与态数提供灵活的目标选择，且具备可证明的收敛性。
