Abstract: Many problems in quantum chemistry, many-body physics, and optimization require excited states of a Hamiltonian. Double-bracket quantum algorithms compile circuits recursively from evolutions under the Hamiltonian and a second operator, without a parameterized ansatz. They have been proposed for several tasks, such as diagonalization and the preparation of ground and thermal states. In this work, we extend this framework to excited states. Combining the ensemble approach with double-bracket flows, we introduce the Double-Bracket quantum algorithm for Multiple EXcited states (DB-MEX), which evolves $p+1$ orthonormal states toward low-lying eigenstates of the Hamiltonian with a single recursively compiled quantum circuit, so they remain orthonormal by construction. The circuit approximates a double-bracket flow of a weighted sum of projectors onto the evolving states, whose number and weights are design parameters. The number of states sets the highest targeted excited state, and the weights determine whether the algorithm prepares the $p+1$ lowest eigenstates, only the $p$-th excited state, or only the span of the $p+1$ lowest eigenstates. In the latter case, a post-processing step recovers the individual eigenstates from their span. We show that the double-bracket flow and, for small step sizes, its discretization converge to the target for almost all initial states, with explicit local rates given by products of weight and energy gaps.

</details>


### [83] [Time-Travel Paradox in a Bohmian Extension of Deutsch's Approach: Mandatory Tunneling as the "Banana Peel Mechanism"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22794)
*Vladimir Nikitin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文在de Broglie-Bohm理论框架下推广Deutsch的自洽性方法，对粒子本体构型也施加自洽性要求，从而描述单个粒子穿越虫洞回到过去时的自洽演化。在两通道窄带高斯近似下，得到一个必须依靠量子隧穿才能自洽的解，实现了"香蕉皮机制"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自预防悖论（粒子回到过去并阻挡年轻的自己）在一维模型中排除了经典自洽历史；Deutsch量子方案虽通过密度矩阵自洽条件化解矛盾，但其物理诠释有争议，Dunlap指出该模型预设了相互作用的平行世界。作者希望在隐变量理论中给出单个粒子的自洽描述。

Method: 在de Broglie-Bohm理论中扩展Deutsch方法：在量子态密度矩阵自洽条件之外，额外对粒子的本体构型（ontic configuration）施加自洽性要求，使其可讨论单个粒子的自洽演化；并在两通道、窄带高斯近似下求解该自洽条件。

Result: 存在一个自洽解，其中量子隧穿成为必需过程——尽管在普通散射中其概率任意小。这正体现"香蕉皮机制"：为避免回到过去时的矛盾，本应极不可能的事件仍然发生。

Conclusion: 把Deutsch的自洽性从量子态层面扩展到粒子本体构型层面，可在Bohm理论中处理自预防悖论并避免"平行世界"预设；模型还给出的新成果是：自洽性本身会强制要求极低概率的隧穿事件发生。

Abstract: We consider a self-prevention paradox in which a particle travels through a wormhole into its own past, encounters its younger version, and blocks its movement. In a specially constructed one-dimensional model, this collision rules out a classically self-consistent history. Deutsch's quantum approach can resolve such contradictions by imposing a self-consistency condition on the density matrix. The physical interpretation of this approach, however, remains debated. In particular, Dunlap argues that Deutsch's model presupposes the existence of interacting parallel worlds. Here we extend Deutsch's approach within de Broglie-Bohm theory by supplementing the self-consistency condition on the quantum state with a self-consistency requirement for the particle's ontic configuration. This allows us to consider the self-consistent evolution of an individual particle. In a two-channel, narrow-band Gaussian approximation, we find a self-consistent solution in which quantum tunneling is mandatory, despite its arbitrarily small probability in an ordinary scattering process. This realizes the "banana peel mechanism": to avoid a contradiction during a journey into the past, an event that would ordinarily be extremely unlikely occurs nonetheless.

</details>


### [84] [How can every physicist become a Bohmian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22798)
*Hrvoje Nikolić*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将玻姆力学从"微观粒子的本体论"重新诠释为测量仪器宏观指针的有效确定性描述，从而回避其争议性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻姆解释因赋予微观粒子确定性轨迹而备受争议，多数物理学家因此拒斥该纲领；作者希望保留玻姆轨迹的可视化价值，同时剥离其微观本体论承诺。

Method: 概念性重构（reformulation）：不在微观层面应用玻姆力学，而将其作为测量装置宏观指针的"有效确定性描述"（effective deterministic description），对微观世界的根本性质不做任何断言。

Result: 在这一形式下，玻姆轨迹成为展示确定测量结果如何"涌现"的实用可视化工具，其适用范围可扩展至原本拒斥玻姆纲领的物理学家群体。

Conclusion: 玻姆力学的价值不必依赖微观本体论承诺；作为宏观指针的有效描述，它可作为被广泛接受的测量结果确定性涌现的可视化工具。

Abstract: The Bohmian interpretation of quantum mechanics is controversial because it attributes deterministic trajectories to microscopic particles. We propose a reformulation that sidesteps this controversy: rather than applying Bohmian mechanics at the microscopic level, we recast it as an effective deterministic description of the macroscopic pointer of a measuring apparatus, with no claims about the fundamental nature of the microscopic world. In this form, the Bohmian trajectory becomes a practical tool for visualizing the emergence of a definite measurement outcome, broadly acceptable to physicists who would otherwise reject the Bohmian program.

</details>


### [85] [Depth-Dependent Collapse and Warm-Start Limits of a Symmetry-Preserving VQE for the J$_1$--J$_2$ Heisenberg Ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22810)
*Soumyadip Sahoo,Sahinur Reja*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在阻挫自旋链上，VQE的保对称拟设随电路深度出现"双下降"式的非单调行为；链式热启动可消除该崩塌并近乎单调改善，但在强阻挫区仍停滞在约0.50–0.58的保真度——消除深度非单调性并不等于达到基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普遍预期VQE增大电路深度即可改善性能。作者要检验这一预期是否成立，并进一步考察"修正失败"后是否就能保证得到精确基态。

Method: 在N=10与12格的阻挫spin-1/2 J1–J2海森堡环上，采用从Néel态出发的保对称SingleExcitationMinus门拟设，随机初始化并扫描深度L；分析保真度、能量误差、半链纠缠熵与总自旋随深度的变化。诊断机制时利用电路族的嵌套性（nested）与梯度有限性排除表达能力不足与贫瘠高原。再引入链式热启动（chained warm start），即用恒等层扩展已优化的浅层电路。最后在J2/J1=0.60附近（N=10）及0.60–1.00区间（N=12）检验热启动表现。

Result: 随机初始化下，保真度、能量误差、半链熵与总自旋在中间深度（N=10约L≈9；N=12约L=10–12）发生突变，呈现"改善—退化—恢复"的双下降形状。由于拟设嵌套且梯度保持有限，该现象归因于优化失败，而非表达能力丧失或贫瘠高原。链式热启动消除了崩塌，对多数阻挫值使能量误差与保真度近乎单调改善；但在J2/J1≈0.60（N=10）及0.60–1.00（N=12）区间停滞在保真度≈0.50–0.58，尽管能量误差很小且总自旋仍处于S=0扇区。

Conclusion: 更深的电路不一定更好，且修复非单调深度依赖并不等同于达到基态。低能量与类单重态不足以判定成功，保真度、纠缠熵与总自旋需同时满足。结论适用范围限于所研究的系统尺寸与阻挫取值。

Abstract: Increasing circuit depth is expected to improve a variational quantum eigensolver (VQE). We show that this expectation can fail, and that curing the failure does not guarantee the exact ground state. We study a symmetry-preserving ansatz of SingleExcitationMinus gates, started from the Néel state, on the frustrated spin-$1/2$ $J_1$--$J_2$ Heisenberg ring with $N=10$ and $12$ sites. With random initialization the fidelity, energy error, half-chain entropy and total spin change abruptly at intermediate depth ($L\approx 9$ for $N=10$, $L=10$--$12$ for $N=12$), giving improvement, degradation and recovery, a double-descent-like shape. The circuit family is nested and the gradient stays finite, so this is an optimization failure, not a loss of expressibility or a barren plateau. A chained warm start, which extends an optimized shallow circuit with identity layers, removes the collapse and improves energy error and fidelity almost monotonically for most frustration values. However, near $J_2/J_1=0.60$ ($N=10$) and over $J_2/J_1=0.60$--$1.00$ ($N=12$) the warm start stalls at a fidelity of $\approx 0.50$--$0.58$, although the energy error is small and the total spin stays in the $S=0$ sector. Removing the non-monotonic depth dependence is therefore not the same as reaching the ground state, and low energy and a singlet-like state are not sufficient: the fidelity, entropy and total spin must be satisfied together. The conclusions are limited to the system sizes and frustration values studied.

</details>


### [86] [Molecular energies from sparse quantum prolate diagonalization on a trapped-ion quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22834)
*Davide Castaldo,Marvin Kronenberger,Marek Miller,Sélène Forget,Gemma C. Solomon,Kresten Lindorff-Larsen,Matthias Christandl,Markus Reiher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出稀疏量子长球对角化（sparse QPD），将 QPD 与压缩感知结合，利用分子体系哈密顿量谱稀疏性，把采样复杂度由 O(N_s) 降至 O(log N_s)，并在离子阱量子计算机上以化学精度分辨率子能级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 QPD 需对自相关函数的 N_s 个采样点（对应不同线路）逐一测量，采样开销随体系规模线性增长；而分子哈密顿量谱往往高度稀疏，存在利用该稀疏性降低采样成本的动机。

Method: 将原始 QPD 公式与压缩感知重建相结合：对自相关函数进行稀疏采样，利用谱稀疏先验做稀疏信号恢复，从而在保持线路深度的前提下减少所需的不同采样电路数目；并以水环境中精氨酸—色氨酸残基对的两轨道活性空间嵌入哈密顿量为实例，在 Quantinuum Helios 离子阱量子计算机上验证，另对更大体系做数值模拟。

Result: 采样复杂度从 O(N_s) 降至 O(log N_s)，且不增加线路深度即可达到化学精度；在 Helios 上以最多 448 个两比特门的线路实现化学精度下的能量分辨；数值模拟表明在弱关联区间该方法可扩展至更大体系。

Conclusion: 压缩感知可与 QPD 有效融合，利用谱稀疏性显著降低采样开销，并在当前硬件上保持化学精度，具备向更大体系扩展的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce sparse quantum prolate diagonalization (sparse QPD), which combines the original formulation of QPD [Phys. Rev. Res., 8(3), 033177, (2026)] with compressed sensing to exploit Hamiltonian spectrum sparsity for molecular systems. We show that sparse sampling reduces the sample complexity of QPD from $\mathcal{O}(N_s)$ to $\mathcal{O}(\log N_s)$, where $N_s$ is the number of sampled points of the autocorrelation function (i.e.\ distinct circuits), without increasing circuit depth at chemical accuracy. With a two-orbital active-space-embedded Hamiltonian for an interacting arginine--tryptophan residue pair in water as an example, we show that on the Quantinuum Helios trapped-ion quantum computer, sparse QPD resolves the Hamiltonian energies to chemical accuracy with circuits of up to 448 two-qubit gates. For a suitably large quantum processor, we demonstrate numerically in the weakly correlated regime that the method extends to larger systems.

</details>


### [87] [Exact classical bounds of bipartite correlation Bell inequalities using improved branch-and-bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22893)
*István Márton,Erika Bene,Péter Diviánszky,Tamás Vértesi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出改进分支定界(IBB)算法精确计算二分关联Bell不等式的经典(局部)界，用see-saw启发式下界与行重排序收紧剪枝，OpenMP并行实现显著加速，可处理至130×130方阵及81×321矩形矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分支定界算法在精确计算二分关联Bell不等式的经典(局部)界时搜索开销大，需通过更强剪枝与并行化提升可解规模。

Method: 在标准BB算法上做两点强化：(1)基于see-saw迭代的启发式搜索提供强初始下界；(2)对Bell矩阵进行贪心行重排序以降低精确搜索中使用的尾部界(tail bounds)。并以OpenMP实现共享内存并行与动态任务再分配。

Result: 随机矩阵基准显示运行时间大幅下降；显式构造实现方阵至130×130、矩形至81×321的精确局部界计算；应用于d=3,4,5,7得到改进的有限阶Grothendieck常数下界，并给出量子-经典比超过√2的均匀输入XOR博弈。

Conclusion: 该方法仍具指数最坏情形搜索复杂度(问题为NP难)，实际性能依赖矩阵结构与尾部界预处理深度，但在可解规模与运行效率上均有实质提升。

Abstract: We present an improved branch-and-bound (IBB) algorithm for the exact computation of classical (local) bounds of bipartite correlation Bell inequalities. We strengthen pruning in the standard BB algorithm in two ways: a heuristic search based on see-saw iteration supplies a strong initial lower bound, and greedy row reordering of the Bell matrix reduces the tail bounds used during the exact search. An OpenMP implementation combines these improvements with shared-memory parallelism and dynamic work redistribution. Random-matrix benchmarks show substantial runtime reductions in matrix sizes, while explicit constructions demonstrate exact local-bound computations for square matrices up to $130 \times 130$ and rectangular matrices of sizes up to $81 \times 321$. Applications provide improved lower bounds on finite-order Grothendieck constants for $d=3,4,5,7$ and uniform-input XOR games with quantum-to-classical ratios exceeding $\sqrt2$. The method retains an exponential worst-case search for this NP-hard optimization problem, and its practical performance depends on matrix structure and tail-bound preprocessing depth.

</details>


### [88] [Development of a Discrete Variable Representation of a Field-Adaptive Hermite Basis for Accurate Modeling of Magnetically Confined Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22906)
*Athul Tom,Pratyush Bhattacharjya,Sangita Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出磁场依赖的Hermite-B DVR方法，以统一波函数表示覆盖Zeeman到Landau区间，并引入空间限制方案，在二维量子点基准中精度高且基函数数最少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场下谱演化需要一种能跨不同磁场区间一致描述多态的共同波函数表示；传统DVR通常在无场基组下处理磁约束体系，难以同时兼顾Zeeman区到Landau区的精度与任意非解析限制势的灵活表示。

Method: 构建磁场依赖的Hermite函数基DVR（Hermite-B），使基函数自然适配磁约束、随场强压缩并保持在标量限制势相关区域中心；动能仍按基组精确解析求解，势能则采用实空间灵活表示以兼容任意非解析势。另提出空间限制方案，将Hermite DVR格点约束于固定长度区域，实现光滑势的紧凑表示。以垂直磁场下二维量子点为例，与解析模型、有限网格法、sine DVR及无场Hermite DVR进行基准比较，并首次系统考察场无关DVR方法在磁约束体系中的表现。

Result: 与精确解析谱和波函数高度一致；Hermite-B所需基函数最少、哈密顿矩阵更小，计算效率更高；对任意限制势与倾斜磁场的应用验证了其超越可解析体系的通用性。

Conclusion: Hermite-B DVR为外磁场下真实半导体纳米结构与分子体系的大规模计算提供了稳健、精确且高效的框架。

Abstract: Accurate magnetic-field evolution of spectra requires a common wavefunction representation that consistently describes multiple states across different field regimes. Discrete Variable Representation (DVR) methods combine accurate basis-based kinetic-energy evaluation with flexible real-space potential representation, enabling arbitrary non-analytic potentials to be treated. Here, we develop a magnetic-field-dependent Hermite function-based (Hermite-B) DVR that adapts naturally to magnetic confinement, efficiently representing compressed wavefunctions across a wide range of field strengths while remaining centered in regions relevant to the scalar confinement potential. This ensures accurate treatment of the field-dependent kinetic energy while addressing two key challenges: maintaining accuracy from the Zeeman to Landau regimes and accommodating diverse confinement potentials. We also introduce a spatial restriction scheme that confines Hermite DVR grid points to a fixed-length domain, enabling compact representations for smooth potentials. The method is benchmarked for two-dimensional quantum dots in perpendicular magnetic fields against analytical models and compared with finite-grid, sine DVR, and field-free Hermite DVR calculations. The performance of field-independent DVR methods for magnetically confined systems is also investigated for the first time. Excellent agreement with exact analytical spectra and wavefunctions is obtained, with Hermite-B requiring the fewest basis functions and smaller Hamiltonian matrices, improving computational efficiency. Applications to arbitrary confinement potentials and tilted magnetic fields demonstrate its versatility beyond analytically solvable systems. The proposed method offers a robust, accurate, and efficient framework for large-scale calculations of realistic semiconductor nanostructures and molecular systems in external magnetic fields.

</details>


### [89] [Entanglement cost and classical capacity of multimode Gaussian states and channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22915)
*Yuan Gao,Gerardo Adesso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明任意有限模连续变量系统下,有限能量双分高斯态的纠缠形成、高斯纠缠形成与纠缠代价三者相等,其公共值强超可加,并由此确定任意多模高斯信道的纠缠代价及平均能量约束下的经典容量,同时给出四模bound entangled态纠缠代价的封闭公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯态与高斯操作是连续变量量子信息与通信的核心,但针对任意有限模高斯态,纠缠形成、高斯纠缠形成与纠缠代价是否一致、以及多模高斯信道的纠缠代价与容量是否可解析求解,尚缺乏统一结论。

Method: 结合纯高斯态纠缠的测地凸性(geodesic convexity)与任意态的相对熵不等式进行证明;将信道纠缠代价与经典容量化归为高斯协方差优化问题。

Result: 有限能量双分高斯态的纠缠形成、高斯纠缠形成与纠缠代价三者相等,公共值强超可加,并给出同协方差矩阵的任意非高斯态相应量的下界;任意多模高斯信道的纠缠代价与平均能量约束下的经典容量均被确定,容量由纯高斯信号加高斯位移调制、跨信道使用时独立实现;得到一类四模bound entangled态纠缠代价的封闭公式。

Conclusion: 高斯框架下的纠缠度量在高斯态上完全一致且具强超可加性,可作为非高斯态的下界;高斯信道资源与容量问题可归约为协方差优化并获解析解。

Abstract: Gaussian states and operations are central to continuous-variable quantum information and communication. For systems with any finite number of modes, we prove that entanglement of formation, Gaussian entanglement of formation, and entanglement cost coincide for every finite-energy bipartite Gaussian state. Their common value is strongly superadditive and lower-bounds entanglement of formation and entanglement cost for arbitrary non-Gaussian states with the same covariance matrix. We then determine the entanglement cost of every multimode Gaussian channel as well as its classical capacity under a mean energy constraint. These quantities reduce to Gaussian covariance optimizations, and the capacity is attained by pure Gaussian signals with Gaussian displacement modulation, independently across channel uses. The proof combines geodesic convexity of entanglement for pure Gaussian states with a relative-entropy inequality for arbitrary states. We also obtain a closed formula for the entanglement cost of a family of four-mode bound entangled states.

</details>


### [90] [Problem-adjusted $X$-mixer eigenstates for improved QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22916)
*Matvii Kistaiev,Georgios Korpas,Bart Partoens,Matthias Van der Donck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将"问题定制的X-mixer激发本征态"作为QAOA初始态，证明在基态简并问题上可提升性能，且在门噪声与读出噪声下优势仍保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准QAOA采用X-mixer基态（均匀叠加态）初始化，对具有简并基态的问题可能并非最优起点；作者希望探究问题定制的激发本征态能否带来性能增益，并考察其在实际噪声条件下的稳健性。

Method: 以X-mixer哈密顿量的问题调整激发本征态替代标准基态初始化，在含门噪声与读出噪声的模拟中比较二者最优性能；分析优势对QAOA层数及初始态到问题哈密顿量基态空间是否存在合理时间演化路径的依赖；给出精确制备这些本征态的方法，以及用阶梯算符线性组合进行近似的方法；并借助Grover型mixer哈密顿量将该思路推广到任意（非简并）问题哈密顿量。

Result: 问题调整的激发初始态在简并基态问题上优于标准基态初始化，且该优势在存在门噪声和读出噪声时依然保持；增益大小取决于QAOA层数与初始态是否具备通向基态空间的时间演化路径。

Conclusion: 问题定制的X-mixer激发本征态是一种有效的QAOA初始化策略，可提升简并基态问题的优化性能且在噪声下稳健，并可通过Grover型mixer推广至一般问题哈密顿量。

Abstract: We show that the use of problem-adjusted excited eigenstates of the $X$-mixer Hamiltonian can be beneficial for the performance of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) when applied to problems with degenerate ground states. We show that this advantage persists in the presence of quantum gate and readout noise, with the problem-adjusted excited initial states achieving better optimal performance than the standard ground state initialization. The realized advantage depends on the number of QAOA layers and whether or not the initial state has a reasonable time evolution path into the ground space of the problem Hamiltonian. We also explain how to prepare these problem-adjusted $X$-mixer eigenstates exactly, as well as how to approximate them using a linear combination of ladder operators. Finally, we discuss how the power of these initial states can be applied to any problem Hamiltonian regardless of the degeneracy of its ground state by making use of a Grover-style mixer Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [91] [Dynamical Decoupling via Continuous Unitary Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22933)
*Xiaodong Yang,Jun Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于连续幺正设计的动力学解耦框架，在连续控制下抑制任意静态噪声，并给出抑制Rabi幅度误差的闭式相位条件；在超导量子云平台验证，相干时间显著延长且鲁棒性最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力学解耦依赖理想瞬时脉冲假设，在有限脉宽与非理想控制的现实条件下性能受限。

Method: 构建基于连续幺正设计（continuous unitary designs）的动力学解耦框架，在连续控制过程中内生地抑制任意静态噪声；推导闭式相位条件以抑制Rabi幅度误差，将普适解耦、有限脉冲兼容性与控制误差鲁棒性统一于同一解析框架。

Result: 在超导量子云平台上完成实验验证，相干时间显著延长；在测试序列中，对Rabi幅度误差与失谐的鲁棒性最高。

Conclusion: 连续幺正设计型动力学解耦可摆脱瞬时脉冲理想化限制，在单一解析框架内同时实现普适噪声抑制与有限脉冲条件下的控制误差鲁棒性，具备实际平台可行性。

Abstract: Dynamical decoupling is a cornerstone of quantum control for protecting and engineering quantum systems. However, conventional schemes rely on idealized instantaneous pulses, limiting their performance under realistic finite-duration and imperfect controls. To overcome this limitation, we establish a dynamical decoupling framework based on continuous unitary designs, which intrinsically suppresses arbitrary static noise during continuous control. We further derive closed-form phase conditions to suppress Rabi-amplitude errors, unifying universal decoupling, finite-pulse compatibility, and control-error robustness within a single analytical framework. We experimentally validate our approach on a superconducting quantum cloud platform, demonstrating significantly extended coherence times and the highest robustness against Rabi-amplitude errors and detuning among the tested sequences.

</details>


### [92] [Variable-length MDI QKD under source imperfections and local randomization over horizontal links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23000)
*Gabriele Staffieri,Martí Sales-Moragues,Carlos Pascual-García*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在MDI-QKD中引入基于保真度界的源不完美模型，采用MEAT框架建立变长通用安全性，用圆锥优化估计Rényi熵，实现n~3×10^4轮的小块密钥提取，并通过自适应选取发送振幅补偿信道衰减不均，使商用卫星系统支持>80 km通信距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD的安全性需在器件不完美与有限码长效应下仍成立；MDI-QKD虽可去除探测端可信假设，但Alice/Bob源端不完美仍缺乏与有限码长通用安全性相容的严格刻画，尤其在小块长与星地非对称信道场景下性能受限。

Method: 构建Alice与Bob光源的保真度界不完美模型，与MDI-QKD的内禀不可信探测相结合；基于Marginal-constrained Entropy Accumulation Theorem（MEAT）建立变长通用安全性证明；借助圆锥优化（conic optimization）估计所需Rényi熵；在水平信道链路下进行数值评估，并针对非对称衰减配置优化发送振幅。

Result: 在无大气条件下，块长小至n~3×10^4轮仍可提取安全密钥；通过恰当选择发送振幅补偿不等信道衰减，非对称配置性能显著提升，商用卫星系统可实现超过80 km的通信距离。

Conclusion: 将源端保真度不完美模型、MEAT变长通用安全性与圆锥优化熵估计相结合的MDI-QKD分析框架，可在有限码长下给出严格安全密钥率，并借由振幅优化拓展星地等非对称链路的可用距离。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) provides security guarantees that hold despite device imperfections and finite-size effects. We address these challenges in measurement-device-independent (MDI) QKD, combining its intrinsic untrusted detection with an imperfection model for Alice's and Bob's sources via fidelity bounds. We establish variable-length general security under the Marginal-constrained Entropy Accumulation Theorem (MEAT), estimating the relevant Rényi entropies through conic optimization. We evaluate our model under a horizontal channel link, reporting secret keys for blocks as small as n~3x10^4 rounds under atmosphere-free conditions, and show that compensating for unequal channel attenuation through appropriately chosen transmitted amplitudes improves performance in asymmetric configurations, enabling communication distances beyond 80 km for commercial satellite systems.

</details>


### [93] [PRISM: A Logical-Physical Boundary Compiler for Dual-Rail Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23009)
*Evan McKinney,Kun Liu,Harshvardhan K. Babla,Robert J. Schoelkopf,Yongshan Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: PRISM 将逻辑门实现方式的选择推迟到布线阶段，跨逻辑-物理边界保留多种等价实现，从而显著降低 SWAP 数与物理深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个逻辑门可有多种通过不同线路交互的等价物理实现，若在布线前就固定实现，后续布线可能被迫引入 SWAP 而另一种实现可避免，形成编译中"代码展开与物理布线"的阶段顺序问题。

Method: PRISM 在逻辑-物理边界上保持每个门的实现选项开放，直到布线到达可执行该门的布局才提交；形式化了哪些物理线路实现该门、哪些满足故障模型、编译器保留哪些，并定义门在某布局下可执行的条件。对双轨腔量子比特，逻辑 CR_z 有四种无损耗实现的轨对配对，其逻辑可用性图正是占用设备图按轨对的简单商图。

Result: 在三种设备图、7–25 逻辑比特的 25 个工作负载上，相比布线前固定实现的贪心搜索，SWAP 减少 20.4–30.8%，物理深度降低 8.8–10.1%；在网格与六角格上 SWAP 降幅在 32 逻辑比特内仍成立。因路径缩短，器件模型上光子损耗降低，16 逻辑比特时每次保留采样的尝试次数几何平均减少 44.3%，并降低三轮 Steane 存储中 skip-gate 泄漏下的解码逻辑错误。

Conclusion: 将门实现选择延后至布线决策可有效缓解阶段顺序问题，在路由开销、深度与硬件级损耗/逻辑错误上均带来一致收益。

Abstract: Quantum computers can encode each logical qubit across multiple physical degrees of freedom so that errors on the physical qubits can be detected or corrected. A compiler must then translate each logical gate into native instructions, and one gate can have several equivalent realizations that interact through different wires. Routing moves those wires, so a realization chosen before routing can force SWAPs that another would avoid, making code expansion and physical routing a phase-ordering problem. PRISM therefore keeps each gate's realizations open across the logical--physical boundary and commits to one only when routing reaches a layout that can execute it. We formalize which physical circuits implement a gate, which respect the fault model, and which the compiler keeps, and define when a gate can execute under a layout. For dual-rail cavity qubits, logical $\mathrm{CR}_z$ has four loss-admissible realizations, one per rail pairing, and its logical availability graph is exactly the simple quotient of the occupied device graph by the rail pairs. Across 25 workloads with 7 to 25 logical qubits on three device graphs, PRISM reduces routing SWAPs by 20.4--30.8\% and physical depth by 8.8--10.1\% relative to a greedy search that fixes each realization before routing. On the grid and hexagonal lattice, the SWAP reductions hold through 32 logical qubits. By shortening routes, PRISM also reduces photon loss on device models and therefore needs 44.3\% fewer attempts per retained shot at 16 logical qubits, as a geometric mean over workloads and device graphs, and it lowers decoded logical error in a three-round Steane memory under skip-gate leakage.

</details>


### [94] [Frequency Multiplexing of Purcell-Enhanced Optically Coherent NV Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23046)
*Alok Gokhale,Marco E. Stucki,Malo Bezard,Maarten H. van der Hoeven,Kilian Unterguggenberger,Gregor Pieplow,Tommaso Pregnolato,Julian M. Bopp,Laura Orphal-Kobin,Tim Schröder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将多个近寿命极限相干性的NV色心集成到单片金刚石光子晶体纳米腔中，实现19 MHz窄线宽、腔内频率复用与Purcell增强发射，为可扩展量子网络节点提供平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将光学相干量子发射体集成进纳米光子结构是可扩展量子网络的关键，但金刚石NV色心在纳米加工过程中光学相干性会退化，成为主要障碍。

Method: 把多个NV色心集成进单片金刚石光子晶体纳米腔，在保持近寿命极限光学相干性的同时测量线宽；将腔模与NV零声子线共振调谐，并用有限元模拟验证Purcell增强。

Result: 观测到窄至19 MHz的线宽，可在同一腔模内对谱上可分辨的多个NV色心进行频率复用；共振激发下电荷态稳定性维持数分钟；腔与零声子线共振时发射增强，与有限元模拟预测的Purcell增强一致；同一芯片上各腔可独立调谐至NV零声子线共振。

Conclusion: 该工作将类体材料的光学相干性、频率复用与腔增强发射相结合，确立了面向NV基量子网络节点的可扩展纳米光子平台。

Abstract: Integrating optically coherent quantum emitters into nanophotonic structures is key to scalable quantum networks. Realizing them with nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond is hindered by the degradation of optical coherence accompanying nanofabrication. Here, we overcome this challenge by integrating multiple NV centers into a monolithic diamond photonic crystal nanocavity while preserving near-lifetime-limited optical coherence. We observe linewidths as narrow as 19 MHz, enabling the frequency multiplexing of spectrally distinct NV centers within a common cavity mode, while maintaining charge-state stability over several minutes under resonant excitation. Tuning the cavity into resonance with the NV zero-phonon line enhances the optical emission, consistent with Purcell enhancement predicted by finite-element simulations. Additionally, individual cavities can be independently brought into resonance with NV zero-phonon lines on the same chip. These results combine bulk-like optical coherence with frequency multiplexing and cavity-enhanced emission, establishing a scalable nanophotonic platform for NV-based quantum network nodes.

</details>


### [95] [Performance of Fusion-Based Quantum Computing with Imperfect Photon Indistinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23083)
*Katia Hakem,Grégoire de Gliniasty,Nathan Shettell,Paul Hilaire,Stephen C. Wein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究部分光子可区分性对融合型量子计算(FBQC)容错的影响，发现逻辑编码对光子损耗的优势并不能普遍延伸到可区分性噪声；容忍度强烈依赖资源态生成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FBQC是容错光量子计算的有前景路径，逻辑编码被认为能显著提升对光子损耗的鲁棒性，但这种收益是否能推广到其他噪声（尤其是资源态生成与融合测量中的部分光子可区分性）尚不清楚，且以往评估多基于理想化资源态假设。

Method: 构建硬件驱动的噪声模型来刻画部分光子可区分性，在6-ring融合网络中比较未编码与Shor(2,2)编码的资源态，涵盖全光子(all-photonic)与发射器辅助的repeat-until-success(RUS)两种制备方案。

Result: 全光子构造下，编码使可区分性阈值从0.136%降至0.08%；RUS构造容忍更高的可区分性，未编码与编码阈值相当（0.256%与0.271%）。

Conclusion: 在理想化资源态假设下评估逻辑编码存在根本局限；资源态生成方案能决定性影响光子架构的容错性能，编码带来的收益并非普遍成立。

Abstract: Fusion-based quantum computation (FBQC) is a promising route to fault-tolerant photonic quantum computing, connecting small entangled resource states through linear-optical fusion measurements. While logical encoding can substantially improve resilience to photon loss, whether these benefits extend to other noise remain an open question. In this work, we show that this is not generally the case when accounting for partial photon distinguishability during resource state generation and fusion measurement. We develop hardware-motivated noise models to capture these effects and investigate their impact on fault tolerance in the 6-ring fusion network. We compare unencoded and Shor(2,2)-encoded resource states prepared using all-photonic and emitter-assisted repeat-until-success (RUS) schemes. For the all-photonic construction, encoding reduces the distinguishability threshold from 0.136% to 0.08%. In contrast, the RUS construction tolerates more distinguishability, with comparable unencoded and encoded thresholds (0.256% and 0.271%, respectively). These results expose a fundamental limitation of evaluating logical encodings under idealized resource-state assumptions and demonstrate that resource-state generation can critically determine the fault tolerance of photonic architectures.

</details>


### [96] [Causality, determinism and predictability: Probing nature through classical and quantum eyes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23128)
*Mundur V N Murthy,Usha Devi A R*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 文章面向学生与感兴趣的读者，梳理从经典世界观到量子描述中因果性、决定论与可预测性概念如何被改变，并回顾爱因斯坦、玻尔等物理学巨人的争论及遗留的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学中常缺少一条清晰的线索，说明经典物理与量子物理在因果性、决定论和可预测性上的概念差异；且量子力学形成后物理学界的著名争论及其当代遗留问题值得系统梳理，以帮助入门读者建立正确概念框架。

Method: 概念性与教学性综述：先阐释经典动力学中决定论与可预测性如何运作，并勾勒因果性概念；再讨论量子力学框架下这些概念如何被重新审视、甚至放弃；随后以历史与思想史视角回顾爱因斯坦、玻尔、海森堡、泡利等人围绕量子力学的争论。

Result: 部分问题在20世纪内已按量子世界观得到解决（如对决定论式隐变量的限制），但仍有许多开放问题，其中最重要的是引力在量子框架中塑造事件所扮演的角色。

Conclusion: 从经典到量子存在范式转变：决定论与可预测性不再是普遍成立的前提，严格因果性也需重新界定；量子世界观已被部分证据支持，但引力与量子事件的结合仍是核心未解问题。

Abstract: In this article, mainly focussed on students and interested readers, we discuss how the understanding of causality, determinism and predictability is transformed as we move from the classical world view to the quantum description. We first explain how determinism and predictability operate in classical dynamics and outline the notion of causality. A paradigm shift occurs within the framework of quantum mechanics where we have to revisit, and even give up, the notions of determinism, predictability and maybe even strict causality. An engrossing and fascinating debate ensues, after the formulation of quantum mechanics among the giants of physics like Einstein, Bohr, Heisenberg, Pauli, and many others. While some questions have been settled in the last century in favour of quantum world view, there are still many open questions, most important of which is the role of gravity in shaping events within the quantum framework.

</details>


### [97] [Teleportation of Logical Qubits using Lattice Surgery with Superconducting Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23165)
*Luca Hofele,Yongxin Song,Áron Márton,Ian Hesner,Ilya Besedin,Jakob Ekert,Jonathan Trebst,François Swiadek,Felix Henrich,Dante Colao Zanúz,Mohsen Bahrami Panah,Lukas Bödeker,Nicholas Gialouris,Chrysander D. Hagen,Marek Pechal,Jean Claude Besse,Markus Müller,Christoph Hellings,Andreas Wallraff*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在41个物理量子比特的超导电路中实验实现容错格点手术（lattice surgery），完成两个距离3逻辑比特间的逻辑态传态，并验证重复稳定子测量可恢复容错性、提升传态保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子计算需通过量子纠错克服控制精度与相干性限制，实现容错逻辑比特及逻辑门。格点手术是表面码中实现逻辑比特间操作的关键原语，但其容错性与优化策略缺乏系统性实验刻画。

Method: 基于超导电路（41个物理比特）构建两个距离3的表面码逻辑比特，实验实现容错格点手术：通过合并码片（merge）并重复执行稳定子测量，跟踪逻辑可观测量随时间的演化，将逻辑态从源逻辑比特传态到目标逻辑比特；同时比较不同门数与时序下的协议优化方案。

Result: 合并码片后重复执行稳定子测量可恢复容错性，使传态态保真度提高；在优化门数与序列时长的协议下观察到保真度进一步提升；并完整给出所用物理器件与门序列的描述。

Conclusion: 工作为容错格点手术操作的优化提供新见解，表明高效实现日益复杂的逻辑电路将提升未来容错量子计算机的性能。

Abstract: Demonstrating a quantum advantage when solving large-scale computational problems on a quantum computer requires the correction of errors that occur due to limitations in control accuracy and coherence. Using the concept of quantum error correction, fault-tolerant logical qubits can be realized and manipulated with logical single- and two-qubit gates. In this paper, we experimentally analyze fault-tolerant lattice-surgery, which is a key primitive that enables operations between logical qubits realized in the surface-code. Using this primitive, we demonstrate the teleportation of logical states between two distance-three logical qubits realized in a superconducting circuit composed of 41 physical qubits. Throughout this process, we study the step-by-step transfer of logical information from a source to a target logical qubit by characterizing the time evolution of logical observables. We find that repeated execution of stabilizer measurements, after merging two surface-code patches, restores fault tolerance and yields increased fidelity of the teleported state. When optimizing the protocol in terms of gate count and sequence duration, we observe further improvements in fidelity. Our work offers new insights into the optimization of fault-tolerant lattice-surgery operations, while also providing a comprehensive description of the physical device and gate sequences used. We expect the efficient implementation of increasingly complex logical circuits to enhance the performance of future fault-tolerant quantum computers.

</details>


### [98] [Integral Tensor Isomorphism via Cubic Rings and Lattice Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23186)
*Thomas Huffstutler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了$\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Z})\times\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{Z})\times\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{Z})$下整$2\times3\times3$张量同构问题可在BQP（有界误差量子多项式时间）内判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数环上张量的同构判定是格/模论与计算复杂性交叉的核心问题；此前对高维（如$2\times3\times3$）情形缺乏明确的算法复杂性刻画。

Method: 对行列式形式$\det(xA-yB)\neq0$的张量，用Wood关于balanced modules的参数化，将同构问题归约为相容格的principal（主格）判定，再用Bley–Hofmann–Johnston的算法在BQP内求解。当行列式形式恒为零时，分别得到整$2\times2\times2$核心、或一次/二次多项式核向量：线性核情形化为另一相容格问题；二次核情形归约为固定多项式同余方程组与幺模提升，用分位数消元在给定模数素因子分解下确定性多项式时间判定（素因子分解由量子算法提供）。并证明零填充保持整$2\times2\times2$张量同构，从而给出real-quadratic principality到恒奇异$2\times3\times3$问题的多项式时间归约。

Result: 整$2\times3\times3$张量同构在给定模数素因子分解的条件下于BQP内可判定；并建立real-quadratic principality问题到恒奇异$2\times3\times3$张量同构问题的多项式时间归约。

Conclusion: 整$2\times3\times3$张量同构问题属于BQP；其间断情形（恒奇异）与实二次域的主格判定问题存在多项式时间归约，揭示了该张量同构问题与数论判定问题的紧密联系。$2\times2\times2$张量同构在零填充下不变。

Abstract: We prove that isomorphism of integral $2\times3\times3$ tensors under $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Z})\times\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{Z})\times\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{Z})$ is decidable in bounded-error quantum polynomial time. For a tensor $T=(A,B)$ with nonzero determinant form $\det(xA-yB)$, we use the parametrization of Wood by balanced modules to reduce the isomorphism problem to principality tests for compatible lattices, and use the algorithms of Bley, Hofmann, and Johnston to solve this in BQP. When the determinant form vanishes identically, we get either an integral $2\times2\times2$ core, or a polynomial kernel vector of degree one or two. The linear-kernel case yields another compatible lattice problem, while the quadratic-kernel case reduces to a fixed system of polynomial congruences and unimodular lifting. A quantifier-elimination argument decides these congruences in deterministic polynomial time given the prime factorization of the modulus, which quantum integer factorization supplies. Finally, we show that zero padding preserves integral $2\times2\times2$ tensor isomorphism, yielding a polynomial-time reduction from real-quadratic principality to the identically singular $2\times3\times3$ problem.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum amplification with a linear quantum coupler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23188)
*Changqing Wang,Yao Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究基于线性电感耦合器（LINC）的约瑟夫森参量放大器性能，从驱动电路哈密顿量出发推导含通量驱动非线性修正的有效三波混频模型，并分析增益、饱和与互调失真，表明其对称保护结构可抑制静态克尔非线性，通过参数优化还能降低修饰压缩与驱动克尔效应，从而显著提升功率处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约瑟夫森参量放大器（JPA）是量子极限微波测量的关键器件，但其动态范围常受克尔效应及其他泵浦诱导非线性效应限制，亟需提升线性度与功率处理能力的器件架构。

Method: 以LINC（线性电感耦合器）结构为对象，从驱动电路哈密顿量出发，推导有效三波混频放大器模型，并纳入实际工作条件下的主导通量驱动非线性修正；据此分析增益响应、饱和行为与互调失真，并与代表性先进JPA设计进行对比。

Result: LINC的对称保护结构可抑制静态克尔非线性；经适当的电路参数优化，可进一步减弱修饰压缩（dressed-squeezing）与驱动克尔效应，在功率处理指标上获得可观改善。

Conclusion: LINC架构有望成为高线性度约瑟夫森参量放大的理想平台。

Abstract: Josephson parametric amplifiers (JPAs) are essential components for quantum-limited microwave measurement, but their dynamic range is often constrained by Kerr and other pump-induced nonlinear effects. In this work, we systematically study the performance of a parametric amplifier based on the linear inductive coupler (LINC). Starting from the driven-circuit Hamiltonian, we derive the effective three-wave-mixing amplifier model, including leading flux-driven nonlinear corrections relevant to realistic operation. We analyze the resulting gain response, saturation behavior, and intermodulation distortion, comparing them with representative state-of-the-art JPA designs. Our results show that the symmetry-protected structure of the LINC suppresses static Kerr nonlinearity. Moreover, suitable circuit-parameter optimization can further reduce the dressed-squeezing and driven-Kerr effects, leading to considerable improvements in power-handling metrics. These results identify the LINC architecture as a promising platform for high-linearity Josephson parametric amplification.

</details>


### [100] [Performance analysis of a Rydberg atom THz radiometer concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23193)
*Richard J. Murchie,Petronilo Martin-Iglesias,Julian Hofmann,Christophe Buisset*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 评估里德堡原子传感器用于地球观测太赫兹辐射计量的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: THz接收技术处于电子学与光子学之间，成熟度不足，需探索替代方案；里德堡原子传感器对电场高度敏感，且其上/下转换架构已证明可相干转换热辐射，具备室温工作、无需本振等优势。

Method: 基于里德堡原子传感器的上/下转换架构，对其在地球观测背景下用于THz辐射计量的可行性进行技术评估。

Result: 原文未给出具体实验数据或量化结果；摘要仅说明已完成可行性评估。

Conclusion: 里德堡原子传感器作为THz辐射计量的潜在方案具有研究价值，但其在地球观测场景下的实用性需进一步验证。

Abstract: THz receiver technology lies between electronics and photonics; as such, its implementation is not as mature. This warrants investigation into alternative THz receivers such as Rydberg atom sensors, which are highly susceptible to electric fields. In particular, the conversion architecture has been demonstrated to coherently transduce thermal radiation. This system offers room-temperature operation and does not necessarily require a local oscillator at the frequency of the input signal. We provide an assessment of the feasibility of such sensors for THz radiometry within the context of Earth observation.

</details>


### [101] [Nonlinearity-preserving quantum simulation of flows in the early fault-tolerant regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23196)
*Hansol Noh,Unam Oh,Sangkyu Baek,Doyeol,Ahn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将稀疏辨识的非线性动力学低阶多项式模型与Walsh合成的KvN输运及表面码资源估计耦合，评估早期容错量子计算下集合预测的端到端可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合预测需传播概率密度，其线性输运方程保留非线性漂移，但电路代价依赖网格，早期容错量子计算的端到端可行性尚不明确。

Method: 用SINDy拟合多项式降阶模型，接入Walsh合成的Koopman-von Neumann输运电路，并结合表面码资源估计；在固定模型维度与漂移阶数下分析旋转门计数随每降阶坐标网格数的多对数缩放。

Result: 圆柱尾流接近饱和时，相对径向模型1% RMS场误差的制备、传播与采样估计需1.62小时、约56万物理比特；三坐标尾流运行时限从数小时到数周、均低于百万比特。IBM硬件实验实现了该尾流的非线性径向KvN输运，并用参考电路抑制噪声。

Conclusion: 降阶模型多项式阶数可作为早期容错量子模拟的设计变量，在精度与电路代价间取得平衡，使非线性动力学集合预测在百万比特以内具备可行性。

Abstract: Ensemble prediction of nonlinear dynamics propagates a probability density whose linear transport equation retains the nonlinear drift, but grid-dependent circuit costs leave end-to-end feasibility in early fault-tolerant quantum computing unresolved. We couple polynomial reduced-order models fitted by sparse identification of nonlinear dynamics to Walsh-synthesized Koopman-von Neumann (KvN) transport and surface-code resource estimation. At fixed model dimension and drift degree, per-step transport rotation counts scale polylogarithmically with cells per reduced coordinate. Balancing model accuracy against circuit costs makes reduced-model polynomial degree a design variable for early fault-tolerant quantum simulation. For a cylinder wake approaching saturation, preparation, propagation and sampling to 1% relative root-mean-square field error against the radial model are estimated to take 1.62 h on about 560,000 physical qubits. Estimated three-coordinate wake runtimes span hours to weeks below one million qubits. IBM hardware experiments demonstrate nonlinear radial KvN transport for this wake, using reference circuits to mitigate noise.

</details>


### [102] [Modeling quantum information scrambling in realistic protein NMR experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23213)
*Alec F. White,Joseph Haslehurst,Daniel R. Moberg,Justin Provazza,Nicholas C. Rubin,Thomas E. O'Brien,Christian Bengs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构建了从蛋白质分子结构到OTOC动力学的端到端计算流程，在10个蛋白体系、350余对碳-碳自旋对上发现OTOC到达时间与自旋间距近似线性，关联可在毫秒内跨越5–17 Å传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTOC被认为是探测算符扩散与信息置乱的重要工具，并已被提出用于通过NMR提取分子结构信息；但要实现这一潜力，需要在实验相关条件下准确刻画复杂分子环境中的OTOC动力学，此前缺乏从分子结构到OTOC动力学的系统性建模方案。

Method: 建立端到端计算流程：用分子动力学模拟引入分子运动，针对粉末样品作取向平均，并采用实验可实现的双量子有效哈密顿量进行自旋网络截断；在10个蛋白质体系、超过350对碳-碳自旋对（间距5–17 Å）的数据集上计算OTOC动力学，并进行灵敏度分析。

Result: OTOC到达时间随自旋间距近似线性增长，呈现出体系依赖的算符扩散速度，使关联能在毫秒时间尺度上跨越较远距离传播；灵敏度分析表明单个OTOC主要约束单一结构参数，且该参数主要是自旋间距。

Conclusion: OTOC有望在固态NMR实验可行的时间尺度内探测空间上延展的自旋关联，为基于NMR的分子结构信息提取提供理论与建模依据。

Abstract: Out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) are important probes of operator spreading and information scrambling, and have recently been proposed as a means of extracting molecular structural information through nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR). Realizing this potential requires accurate modeling of OTOCs in complex molecular environments within experimentally relevant settings. Here, we develop an end-to-end computational pipeline connecting molecular structures to OTOC dynamics in protein systems. Our framework incorporates molecular motion through molecular dynamics simulations, orientational averaging appropriate for powder samples, and spin-network truncation, using an experimentally realizable double-quantum effective Hamiltonian. We apply this framework to a dataset of over 350 carbon-carbon spin pairs across 10 protein systems, spanning interspin distances of 5-17 A. We find that OTOC arrival times scale approximately linearly with interspin distance, revealing system-dependent operator-spreading velocities that enable correlations to propagate over extended distances within milliseconds. Our sensitivity analysis further shows that individual OTOCs predominantly constrain a single structural parameter, primarily associated with interspin distance. Together, these findings highlight the potential of OTOCs to probe spatially extended spin correlations on timescales compatible with solid-state NMR experiments.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Exact solution of the two-dimensional one-component plasma on the truncated Beltrami pseudosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21652)
*Josep Batle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文精确求解截断Beltrami伪球面（常负曲率、双边界）上Γ=2的二维单组分等离子体，证明配分函数为不完全伽马函数之积、密度显式、关联行列式；大N自由能展开含(1/2)N ln N、线性项、平直硬壁表面张力×边界长及形状常数，无ln N项，逐项与柱面结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验共形反常预测：在欧拉示性数为零、但积分高斯曲率与边界曲率分别非零且相互抵消的曲面上，验证自由能展开中缺失 ln N 项、以及普适常数可由边界层求积给出的预言；此前缺乏此类"曲率与边界贡献分离"的精确可解例子。

Method: 取截断Beltrami伪球面（两平行线截出的常负曲率面），经共形映射化为平直柱面，曲率仅通过面积元进入；在Γ=2下配分函数化为不完全伽马函数乘积，密度显式，所有关联为行列式（可积结构）。面积有界故在固定形状下取大N极限，用常数项逐项闭式求得自由能；并将渐近展开与N≤4096的精确解比较，另做Γ=1、4、6的蒙特卡洛模拟，以Γ=2精确密度作验证。

Result: 自由能 = (1/2)N ln N + 线性N项 + 平直硬壁表面张力×总边界长 + 形状依赖常数，无ln N项；与Shen–Forrester柱面结果逐项吻合，其普适常数为两个边界层的求积。两侧壁处密度分别从相反方向趋于平直接触值 n_b ln 2，尽管周长相差因子cosh u_max。渐近式与N≤4096精确解一致；蒙特卡洛显示边界层随耦合演化。

Conclusion: 在欧拉示性数为零但曲率与边界贡献各自非零的曲面上，共形反常预测得到精确验证；Γ=2的可积结构给出闭式自由能与行列式关联，并确立边界层普适常数的求积表述，为曲率—边界竞争提供可解基准。

Abstract: We solve exactly the two-dimensional one-component plasma at coupling $Γ=2$ on the truncated Beltrami pseudosphere, a surface of constant negative curvature bounded by two parallels. A conformal map onto a flat cylinder leaves the particles interacting through the flat-cylinder potential, with the curvature entering only through the area element. The partition function becomes a product of incomplete gamma functions, the density is explicit, and all correlations are determinantal. Since the area is bounded, the large-N limit is taken at fixed shape. The free energy, obtained through the constant term with every coefficient in closed form, contains a bulk term (1/2)N ln N, a term linear in N, the flat hard-wall surface tension times the total boundary length, and a shape-dependent constant, with no ln N term. This is the conformal-anomaly prediction for Euler characteristic zero, tested here on a surface where the integrated Gaussian and boundary curvatures are separately nonzero and cancel. The expansion coincides term by term with the cylinder result of Shen and Forrester, whose universal constants are quadratures of the two boundary layers. At both walls the density tends to the flat contact value $n_b$ ln 2, from opposite sides, although the circumferences differ by a factor cosh u_max. We compare the asymptotics with the exact solution up to N=4096, and Monte Carlo simulations at $Γ= $1, 4 and 6, validated against the exact $Γ= $ 2 density, show how the boundary layers evolve with coupling.

</details>


### [104] [Renormalization-Group Foundation of Critical Modes in Correlation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21676)
*Yanhua Zhang,Mingmei Xu,Jinghua Fu,Yuanfang Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出基于重整化群标度算符的框架，系统构造临界涨落可观测量族及其连通关联矩阵；本征态给出多尺度临界关联层级，本征值呈有限尺寸标度，并在二维Ising模型的自旋与能量扇区得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界涨落的高阶关联（连通关联矩阵）缺乏系统构造与分类框架，且不易与重整化群标度理论对接；同时希望框架可用于未知哈密顿量体系。

Method: 基于重整化群理论的标度算符，构造完整的临界敏感涨落可观测量族及其连通关联矩阵；对关联矩阵做本征分解，提取多尺度临界关联模式层级结构与归一化本征值及比值的有限尺寸标度行为；在二维Ising模型上，对自旋与能量算符分别构建关联矩阵进行验证。

Result: 能量扇区的本征态模式与领头本征值的标度行为与框架预期一致，为框架提供进一步支持。

Conclusion: 建立了由RG标度算符出发构建临界涨落关联矩阵的通用理论框架，其本征态揭示多尺度临界关联层级、本征值呈有限尺寸标度；并讨论了向未知哈密顿量体系的推广潜力。

Abstract: Based on the relevant scaling operators of renormalization-group theory, we establish a theoretical framework for constructing complete families of critical-sensitive fluctuation observables and their connected correlation matrices. The corresponding eigenstates of the correlation matrices reveal a hierarchy of multiscale critical correlation patterns and the leading normalized eigenvalues and their ratios show the finite-size scaling behavior. Applying this framework to the two-dimensional Ising model, the correlation matrices associated with both spin and energy operators are constructed. The resulting energy-sector eigenstate patterns, together with the scaling behavior of the leading eigenvalues, provide further support for the proposed framework. Finally, we discuss its potential extension to systems with unknown Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [105] [Exact Dynamic Scaling of Quantum Jump Statistics in Monitored Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22029)
*Kazuki Yamamoto,Taiki Ishiyama,Toranosuke Matsubara,Kazuya Fujimoto,Tomohiro Sasamoto,Ryusuke Hamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现受监测多体系统中子系统内量子跳跃数的涨落具有新型动态标度行为：扩散时间尺度与依赖于子系统尺寸的反常超泊松系统尺寸标度；对自由费米子给出Liouvillian自关联函数的精确有限尺寸解，并在大测量强度极限下证明相互作用费米子具有相同标度指数与标度函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量诱导纠缠转变等现象的实验观测通常依赖昂贵的后选择（postselection）。量子跳跃统计提供了无需后选择研究测量诱导多体现象的途径，因此有必要探究测量结果本身是否具有普适性，以建立可实验友好地刻画测量诱导非平衡多体普适性的框架。

Method: 研究受连续监测的多体系统中子系统内量子跳跃数的涨落；对自由费米子，通过求解Liouvillian自关联函数的精确有限尺寸解，精确确定标度指数与标度函数；对相互作用费米子，在大测量强度极限下进行解析分析，检验标度行为的普适性。

Result: 子系统量子跳跃数涨落表现出由扩散时间尺度和反常超泊松系统尺寸标度（依赖于子系统尺寸）刻画的新型动态标度；自由费米子情形下精确给出标度指数和标度函数；相互作用费米子在大测量强度极限下解析证明具有相同的标度指数与标度函数。

Conclusion: 量子跳跃数涨落的动态标度可作为无需后选择、实验友好的观测量，为分类测量诱导非平衡多体普适性提供新框架。

Abstract: Quantum measurement has opened a new frontier in nonequilibrium many-body physics, revealing striking phenomena such as measurement-induced entanglement transitions, whose experimental observation typically requires costly postselection. Quantum jump statistics offer another route to studying such measurement-induced many-body phenomena without postselection, motivating the search for universality in measurement outcomes themselves. In this Letter, we discover that fluctuation of the number of quantum jumps within a subsystem exhibits novel dynamic scaling, characterized by diffusive time scale and anomalous super-Poissonian system-size scaling that depend on the subsystem size in monitored many-body systems. For free fermions under continuous monitoring, we exactly determine the scaling exponent and the scaling function by deriving an exact finite-size solution of the Liouvillian autocorrelation function. We also analytically show that the dynamic scaling with the same scaling exponent and the scaling function even holds for interacting fermions in the limit of large measurement strength. Our work provides a novel framework to classify measurement-induced nonequilibrium many-body universality through experimentally friendly, postselection-free quantities.

</details>


### [106] [Confinement Alters Energy Relaxation in Subrecoil Laser Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22436)
*Daiki Kanbe,Eli Barkai,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在简谐束缚下，亚反冲激光冷却的长时间能量弛豫规律被定性改变：束缚重塑了稀有长俘获事件的相空间几何，导致两级动力学交叉，并使α=4成为平均能量最快渐近衰减的最优散射率指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光冷却需同时降低动能并将原子限制在有限空间区域，但既有亚反冲冷却理论多在自由空间框架下讨论长时间弛豫，未澄清简谐束缚如何改变渐近弛豫律。本文旨在回答：当局域散射律不变时，束缚本身是否、以及通过何种机制改变长时能量弛豫。

Method: 采用随机散射率模型（散射率在低动量处按 R(p)∝|p|^α 消失），结合数值模拟与可解析处理的更新（renewal）近似，分析相空间轨道上位置—动量耦合对长寿命低散射轨迹的影响及俘获时间统计。

Result: 束缚使长俘获事件不再只需小动量，而被限制在相空间原点附近，从而改变俘获时间统计；由此产生两个动力学交叉（crossover），并给出使平均能量渐近衰减最快的最优指数 α=4。

Conclusion: 即使局域散射律保持不变，简谐束缚也会通过相空间几何约束改变亚反冲冷却的渐近能量弛豫动力学。

Abstract: Controlling atomic motion through laser cooling requires both reducing kinetic energy and confining atoms to a finite spatial region. Here, we investigate how harmonic confinement modifies the long-time energy relaxation in subrecoil laser cooling. Using a stochastic scattering-rate model, numerical simulations, and an analytically tractable renewal approximation, we show that confinement qualitatively changes the asymptotic relaxation law. For a scattering rate that vanishes at low momentum as $R(p)\propto |p|^α$, the change arises because confinement reshapes the phase-space geometry of rare, long trapping events. In free space, such events require only small momentum. Under harmonic confinement, however, position and momentum are coupled along phase-space orbits, restricting long-lived low-scattering trajectories to the vicinity of the phase-space origin. This geometric constraint modifies the trapping-time statistics, produces two dynamical crossovers, and leads to an optimal scattering-rate exponent of $α=4$ for the fastest asymptotic decay of the mean energy. Our results demonstrate that confinement changes the asymptotic energy-relaxation dynamics even when the local scattering law remains unchanged.

</details>


### [107] [Bypassing the Entropy Production Speed Limit of State-to-State Transitions with Adaptive Resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22712)
*Daniel B. K. Chu,Rémi Goerlich,Itamar Steinitz,Yael Roichman,Barak Hirshberg*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过引入"自适应重置"这一非局域跳跃输运机制，可突破驱动过程中扩散速度与熵产生的热力学基本下界；混合协议（重置+势场切换）在三类变换中超越扩散界，并给出耦合扩散-重置动力学的新型速度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体与冷原子中的态-态变换（如势阱压缩、比特擦除）通常靠驱动实现，其速度与熵产受一个基本下界约束。作者试图通过更换输运机制而非优化驱动来突破该下界。

Method: 提出自适应重置（adaptive resetting）：利用无重置轨迹学习重置速率，使系统通过非局域跳跃完成态变换；并设计将重置与"切换至目标势"相结合的混合协议；同时推导耦合扩散-重置动力学的速度极限。

Result: 混合协议在三种变换任务中超越了扩散（diffusive）速度/熵产界；并导出了适用于耦合扩散-重置动力学的速度极限关系。

Conclusion: 输运机制的改变（非局域重置）可超越扩散驱动固有的热力学界限，为快速、低耗散的微观态变换提供新途径。

Abstract: State-to-state transitions in colloids and cold atoms (trap compression, bit erasure) are often achieved through driving, whose speed and entropy production are constrained by a fundamental bound. We show that this bound can be surpassed by changing the transport mechanism. Adaptive resetting enables transitions through nonlocal jumps, with rates learned from reset-free trajectories. A hybrid protocol, combining resetting with a switch to the target potential, surpasses the diffusive bound in three transformations. Finally, we derive a speed limit governing coupled diffusion-reset dynamics.

</details>


### [108] [Effects of probe size, shape, and probe-surface interactions in imaging multilayer island growth, coalescence, and formation of a smooth film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22758)
*Ismael S. S. Carrasco,Tiago J. Oliveira,Fábio D. A. Aarão Reis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过KMC模拟与探针-表面相互作用模型证明：AFM测量的C60薄膜粗糙度峰偏低源于探针卷积（接触模式与有限针尖宽度），而非真实形貌差异，并暗示XRR与AFM测量之间存在岛粗化或去润湿等后生长过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱相互作用衬底上沉积的薄膜呈现多层岛状生长及岛合并后的平滑化，粗糙度随厚度出现峰值。但C60薄膜中AFM测得的粗糙度峰显著低于XRR结果，作者认为可能由针尖-样品卷积效应导致，需系统厘清测量伪影与真实形貌演化，并解释两种测量结果的矛盾。

Method: 采用动力学蒙特卡罗（KMC）模拟该类岛状生长薄膜，并构建AFM各成像模式的探针-表面相互作用模型：非接触模式以与针尖宽度L相当的尺度对高度做平均；接触模式分圆柱形探针（硬核相互作用）与尖锐金字塔/圆锥形探针（硬核相互作用）；另建立长方体岛生长与合并的几何模型进行定量对照。

Result: 非接触模式下，当L小于平均岛间距时粗糙度峰仍存在，但峰高随L增大而下降、峰位轻微偏移；圆柱探针接触模式下峰高降低且峰位显著移向更小厚度，表现为"更快的岛合并"；尖锐金字塔/圆锥探针仅在L较大时产生偏差，但此类针尖实验上难以实现。几何模型定量再现了模拟中接触模式与探针形状的影响。

Conclusion: SPM在岛合并生长体系中的成像可能低估膜厚不均匀性；XRR与AFM的C60数据差异更可能来自测量间发生的后生长过程（如岛粗化、去润湿），而非单纯针尖卷积。

Abstract: Recent studies of deposition onto weakly interacting substrates have revealed multilayer island growth, followed by smooth-film formation upon island coalescence. However, for C60 films, the roughness peak measured by atomic force microscopy (AFM) was significantly lower than that obtained by X-ray reflectometry (XRR), suggesting tip-sample convolution effects. We investigate this problem using kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) simulations of such films and models of probe-surface interactions for AFM imaging modes. To model the non-contact mode, surface profiles are transformed by averaging heights over regions matching the probe-tip width. A roughness peak remains if the probe width L is smaller than the average island distance, but its height decreases with L and its position is slightly displaced. In the contact-mode model with cylindrical probes and hard-core interactions, the peak also decreases and shifts significantly to smaller thicknesses, indicating apparently faster island coalescence. Thus, SPM images under these conditions may underestimate film-thickness nonuniformity in deposits with coalescing islands. Instead, models of sharp pyramidal or conical probes with hard-core interactions produce deviations in the roughness peaks only for large L, although such sharp tips are difficult to realize experimentally. A geometric model of cuboid island growth and coalescence quantitatively confirms the effects of contact modes and probe shapes observed in the simulations. The results suggest that post-growth processes, such as island coarsening and dewetting, may have occurred in the recently studied C60 film between the XRR and AFM measurements.

</details>


### [109] [Polymer adsorption on vicinal surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22966)
*Nathann T. Rodrigues,Tiago J. Oliveira*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过大规模模拟研究柔性聚合物链接枝于阶梯边缘的吸附相行为，揭示台阶吸附相、脱附相与平台吸附相之间的连续转变及此前未预期的多临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物在平面（甚至粗糙面）上的吸附理论已较成熟，但实际常见且重要的"近平坦阶梯表面"情形研究不足，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 对嫁接在邻晶面（vicinal surface）台阶边缘的柔性聚合物链进行大规模模拟，系统考察平台宽度 W 与二维回转半径 R_g^(2D) 的比值，并检验台阶扭折（kinks）的影响。

Result: 当 W ≳ 3R_g^(2D) 时，出现稳定的台阶吸附（SA）相，与脱附相、平台吸附（TA）相共存，三者由连续转变线分隔并在一个多临界点相交；当 W ≲ 3R_g^(2D) 时，相图简化为 SA 与 TA 之间的简单交叉（crossover）；台阶扭折不改变上述行为。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了阶梯表面上接枝聚合物的相图框架，提出聚合物热力学中此前未预料的多临界行为，并为利用设计化阶梯表面的台阶吸附/脱附制备聚合物纳米线与调控链构象提供途径。

Abstract: While polymer adsorption is theoretically well established on flat surfaces --- and somewhat understood on rough surfaces --- the common and practically relevant case of nearly flat stepped surfaces remains poorly studied. Here, we address this gap via extensive simulations of flexible polymer chains grafted at the step edge of vicinal surfaces. For wide terraces, the polymer-step interaction yields a stable step-attached (SA) phase, alongside the desorbed and terrace-adsorbed (TA) phases, all of them separated by continuous transition lines that meet at a multicritical point previously unanticipated in polymer thermodynamics. This scenario is observed for terrace widths $\mathcal{W} \gtrsim 3 R_g^{(2D)}$, being $R_g^{(2D)}$ the radius of gyration of TA polymers. Simpler phase diagrams, absent this multicriticality, are found for $\mathcal{W} \lesssim 3 R_g^{(2D)}$, where a simple crossover exists between the SA and TA phases. The presence of kinks at the steps does not alter these behaviors. Besides their theoretical relevance, these findings unveil an interesting route to produce polymer nanowires and control polymer conformation via step attachment-detachment on designed stepped surfaces.

</details>


### [110] [Dynamical theory of the East model. II. A path-probability formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23008)
*Chong Zha,Yuliang Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于路径概率密度建立East模型（一维层级约束动能Ising模型）的动力学理论，并引入系统性的因子化近似使其可解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: East模型缺乏完整严格的动力学理论描述；作者希望以路径概率密度为出发点，建立一个可系统展开、并可推广到其他体系的理论框架。

Method: 以路径概率密度为基本变量，导出单自旋路径概率密度的无穷但封闭方程组，经Laplace变换化为代数差分方程；引入将连续翻转区间联合概率密度因子化为低阶联合密度的系统性近似，并具体实施一阶单区间因子化近似，得到关于persistence函数的四个封闭方程并数值求解。

Result: 所得时间关联函数与弛豫时间在较宽温度范围内与Monte Carlo模拟及Zwanzig–Mori形式的两畴基近似吻合良好；但这两种近似均未能再现低温下精确的超Arrhenius标度行为。

Conclusion: 该形式体系为East模型动力学的完整严格理论描述提供了合理基础，并为推广到其他体系提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: We develop a dynamical theory of the East model---a one-dimensional, hierarchically constrained kinetic Ising model---based on path-probability densities. This theory yields an infinite but closed set of equations for single-spin path-probability densities, which, after Laplace transformation, become algebraic difference equations. To render the theory tractable, we introduce a systematic factorization approximation in which the joint probability densities of consecutive flip intervals are factorized into lower-order joint probability densities. We implement the first-order single-interval factorization approximation, obtain a closed set of four equations for the persistence functions, and solve them numerically. The resulting time correlation functions and relaxation times agree closely with Monte Carlo simulations and with the two-domain-basis approximation of the Zwanzig--Mori formalism over a wide range of temperatures, although both approximations fail to capture the exact low-temperature asymptotic super-Arrhenius scaling of the relaxation time. The present formalism provides a plausible foundation for a complete and rigorous theoretical description of the dynamics of the East model and offers a promising route toward generalization to other systems.

</details>


### [111] [Memory kernel and closure for systems driven by intermittent forcing with power-law waiting times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23092)
*Marco Bianucci,Riccardo Mannella*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究随机时间间隔、随机幅值的间歇控制变量（更新噪声）驱动系统的主方程，给出核系数（G-累积量）的精确分解，并分析重尾等待时间下长滞后核的极限行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇更新噪声下受控变量的主方程含有以噪声G-累积量为系数的记忆核，但其与跳跃统计、更新统计的关系及重尾等待时间下的极限行为缺乏精确刻画。

Method: 对幅值与持续时间独立的任意等待时间分布，推导G-累积量的精确分解（分离跳跃统计与更新统计）；Poisson情形对应跳跃分布的Boolean累积量并可精确求和；对有限均值的幂律等待时间，证明长时滞系数各阶收敛于跳跃律的普通矩，长滞后核成为冻结确定性通量核的叠加，权重为ξ²p(ξ)，并导出闭式主方程。

Result: 给出核系数的精确分解与闭式主方程；对2<μ<3（噪声相关时间无穷），二阶闭合即使在慢动力学下仍保留有限误差，而冻结流主方程在该极限下给出精确的平稳均值；电报过程是唯一使二阶结论精确的更新噪声情形；一个含加性与相关乘性噪声的可解模型验证了上述预测。

Conclusion: 记忆核可被精确分解为跳跃统计与更新统计两部分，重尾更新噪声下长滞后行为由冻结通量核叠加描述；二阶矩闭合一般非精确，仅在电报过程成立，需谨慎使用低阶闭合近似。

Abstract: Many systems are controlled by intermittent control variables that take on a random value for a random time interval. In the presence of such noise at update steps, the main equation for the controlled variables has a memory kernel whose coefficients are the cumulants, also known as $G$-cumulants, of the noise, which are fully ordered in time. In the case of Poisson switching, these correspond to the Boolean cumulants of the jump distribution, and the kernel can be summed exactly. We derive an exact decomposition of these coefficients for any waiting-time distribution with independent amplitudes and durations, which separates the jump statistics from the renewal statistics. In the case of waiting times that follow a power-law distribution with a finite mean, it is highly probable that a long time interval will be covered by a single period, and we have shown that, as a result, the coefficients for long delays intersect in all orders, from Boolean cumulants to the usual moments of the jump law. The long-lag kernel thus becomes a superposition of frozen deterministic flux kernels, weighted by the expression $ξ^2p(ξ)$, and from this the corresponding main equation in closed form follows. For $2<μ<3$, where the noise correlation time is infinite, the second-order closure retains a finite error even in the presence of slow dynamics, whereas the main equation of the frozen flows in this limit yields an exact stationary mean value. The telegraphic process is the only case of noise with renewal steps for which second-order conclusions are exact. An exactly solvable model with additive and correlated multiplicative noise confirms these predictions.

</details>


### [112] [Color Through Time: Distributions, Sparsity and Dominance in Western Painting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23139)
*Ortensia Forni,Alexandre Darmon,Michael Benzaquen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文用大规模数字化画作语料，在感知均匀色彩空间中研究西方绘画色彩的长期演变，提出稀疏度与主导度两个可解释描述符，揭示从暗沉土色向宽域高饱和的转变及"先扩展色域、后增强主导"的两阶段轨迹，并比较艺术调色板与自然图像的偏离方式以及人对色彩和谐平衡的两种判断区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术史中关于绘画色彩演变的论述多为定性描述，缺乏在大规模数据上可量化、可解释的刻画；同时，艺术调色板如何偏离自然色彩统计、以及人类如何判断色彩和谐，尚缺少统一的形式化框架。

Method: 构建大规模数字化画作语料；将每幅画表示为在感知均匀色彩空间中固定调色板上的颜色分布；用Jensen–Shannon距离比较各历史时期与艺术流派之间的色彩分布；引入两个可解释描述符——稀疏度（使用颜色多少）与主导度（颜色分布不均程度）；进一步与自然图像的色彩分布对比，并通过两项生成图像的用户调研考察色彩和谐平衡的判断。

Result: 发现从暗沉土色向更宽广、更高饱和色彩范围的历史性转变，自19世纪起呈现明显分段变化；时期性演变较为平滑，而艺术流派在超越年代顺序后表现出日益鲜明的色彩特征。在稀疏度—主导度平面上，绘画呈两阶段轨迹：先拓宽所用色彩范围，后提升色彩主导度。与自然图像对比显示艺术调色板以"所用颜色"或"分布广度与均匀性"两种不同方式偏离自然。两项调研表明，和谐色彩平衡的判断集中于两个互补区域：多色但强层级，或少色但均匀。

Conclusion: 大规模量化方法可将绘画色彩史表述为可测量、可解释的低维结构；色彩演变并非单一线性过程，而是由色域扩展与色彩主导度提升构成的阶段性轨迹，流派具有独立于年代的色彩签名；艺术对自然的偏离与人类色彩和谐的判断皆呈现二元互补模式，为计算艺术史与色彩感知研究提供统一框架。

Abstract: We investigate the evolution of color in Western painting using a large corpus of digitized artworks. Each painting is represented by its distribution over a fixed palette of colors in a perceptually uniform space. We compare the color distributions of historical periods and artistic movements through their Jensen-Shannon distances, and introduce two interpretable descriptors, sparsity and dominance, that quantify how many colors are used and how unevenly they are represented. We find a historical transition from dark, earthy palettes toward a broader and more saturated chromatic range, unfolding through distinct shifts from the nineteenth century onward. Periods evolve smoothly over time, whereas artistic movements display increasingly distinct chromatic signatures beyond chronology. In the sparsity-dominance plane, painting follows a two-stage historical trajectory, first broadening the range of colors used and later increasing color dominance. Comparison with natural images reveals distinct ways in which artistic palettes depart from nature, either through the colors they use or through how broadly and evenly those colors are distributed. Finally, two surveys on generated images show that judgments of harmonious color balance concentrate around two complementary regimes: many colors arranged in a strong hierarchy, or few colors used evenly.

</details>


### [113] [From nonlocal correlations to local symmetry breaking: Asymmetry dynamics of crosscap states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23224)
*Konstantinos Chalas,Pasquale Calabrese,Colin Rylands*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究charge-deformed crosscap态在自由费米子淬火下的反常动力学：纠缠熵下降而纠缠不对称性增长，即初始对称的子系统会动态地产生局域对称破缺，这一现象由描述对跖关联传播的准粒子图像定量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规认知中，全局对称性守恒的幺正演化会使初始破缺的对称性在局域被恢复、纠缠熵增长。作者希望考察当对称破缺相干性最初以长程纠缠形式编码时，这一图像是否会被反转。

Method: 构造一类电荷形变的crosscap态（由可调纠缠与对称破缺的对跖纠缠对组成），先分析其静态性质（类Page的纠缠轮廓、纠缠不对称性、对称分辨纠缠及子系统连续形变的影响），再淬火到自由费米子紧束缚链，并用准粒子图像描述对跖关联的传播。

Result: 静态上得到类Page的纠缠轮廓；纠缠不对称性在子系统小于系统一半时为零，大于一半时对数增长。淬火后出现非常规动力学：纠缠熵减小而纠缠不对称性增大，初始对称的子系统动态地发展出局域对称破缺，且该行为被准粒子图像定量解释。

Conclusion: 保持对称性的动力学可将全局编码的对称破缺相干性重新分配并使其在局域可及；初始纠缠结构是决定非平衡下局域对称性命运的关键因素。

Abstract: Under unitary dynamics preserving a global symmetry, bipartite entanglement typically grows while an initially broken symmetry is locally restored. Here, we show that this conventional scenario can be reversed when symmetry-breaking coherence is initially encoded in long-range entanglement. We investigate this phenomenon in a family of charge deformed crosscap states, characterized by antipodal entangled pairs with tunable entanglement and symmetry breaking. We first characterize their static properties, finding a Page-like entanglement profile and an entanglement asymmetry that vanishes for subsystems smaller than half the system size, but grows logarithmically for larger subsystems. For symmetric subsystems, we also analyze the symmetry-resolved entanglement and investigate how it evolves as the subsystem is continuously deformed away from a symmetric partition. Following a quench to a free-fermion tight-binding chain, we uncover an unconventional dynamics in which the entanglement entropy decreases whereas the entanglement asymmetry increases, so that initially symmetric subsystems dynamically develop local symmetry breaking. This behavior is quantitatively captured by a quasiparticle picture describing the propagation of antipodal correlations. Our results reveal how symmetry-preserving dynamics can redistribute globally encoded symmetry-breaking coherence, making it locally accessible, and establish the initial entanglement structure as a key ingredient in determining the fate of local symmetries out of equilibrium.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Statistical Properties of the $q$-Deformed Monolayer and Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21675)
*Omar Pedraza,Erik Díaz-Bautista,L. A. López,Daniel O-Campa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究恒定磁场下单层与双层石墨烯中q-变形Dirac振子的统计性质：给出能谱、本征函数与概率流密度，用zeta函数技术与Euler-Maclaurin公式导出配分函数及小变形参数下的主要热力学量，揭示q-变形对能谱、概率流分量和热力学响应的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: q-变形框架已被用于研究石墨烯类体系的量子性质，但单层与双层石墨烯在磁场下q-变形Dirac振子的系统性统计（热力学）分析及其对比尚不充分，需要明确q-变形如何改变能谱结构与热力学响应。

Method: 采用q-变形的产生与湮灭算符求解单层与双层石墨烯的q-变形Dirac振子；在恒定外磁场下获得能谱与本征函数并计算概率流密度，验证未变形极限回归常规Landau能级；对双层体系显式构造最低Landau能级；利用zeta函数技巧结合Euler-Maclaurin公式推导配分函数，并在小变形参数下展开求取主要热力学量。

Result: 未变形极限下能谱回归标准Landau能级结构；q-变形使能谱发生修正，并改变概率流密度各分量的行为；由配分函数得到的热力学量在q-变形下出现修正；单层与双层石墨烯表现出不同的热行为，源于二者Landau能级结构的差异。

Conclusion: q-变形是调控石墨烯Dirac振子能谱与统计性质的有效手段；单层与双层体系因Landau能级结构不同而对q-变形呈现差异化热力学响应，可为相关体系的量子热力学研究提供理论参考。

Abstract: We investigate the statistical properties of the $q$-deformed Dirac oscillator for monolayer and bilayer graphene in the presence of a constant external magnetic field. Using $q$-deformed creation and annihilation operators, we obtain the corresponding energy spectra and eigenfunctions, as well as their probability current densities, recovering the conventional Landau-level structures in the undeformed limit. For bilayer graphene, the lowest Landau levels are explicitly constructed within the $q$-deformed framework. The partition functions for both systems are derived using zeta-function techniques and the Euler-Maclaurin formula, allowing us to determine their main thermodynamic quantities for small values of the deformation parameter. Our results show that the $q$-deformation modifies the energy spectra, the behavior of the probability current density components, and the thermodynamic response of the systems. A comparison between monolayer and bilayer graphene reveals distinct thermal behaviors, reflecting their different Landau-level structures and highlighting the influence of the $q$-deformation on their statistical properties.

</details>


### [115] [The Kitaev model on finite hyperbolic PBC clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21697)
*Alberto Zorzato,Jiri Minář*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在有限双曲格子上构造并求解三色Kitaev模型，用三角形群的有限商生成紧致高亏格曲面上的正则映射以提取格点连接、plaquette结构、同调圈与Wilson扇区，并在{8,3}、{12,3}格子上做有限尺寸标度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Kitaev自旋液体物理推广到双曲（负曲率）格子，并建立一套可系统实现周期边界条件的有限尺寸数值框架，以便在有限体系上检验自旋液相的超选择扇区、通量背景与任意子物理。

Method: 利用保持定向的三角形群之有限商（finite quotients）生成亏格g紧致曲面上的正则映射，从中提取格点连通性、plaquette结构、同调循环与Wilson扇区，并施加一致边界条件；在{8,3}与{12,3}格子上枚举至多72个顶点的若干商作为有限尺寸序列；在 isotropic 点确定基态超选择扇区，量化其与均匀π通量扇区的偏离，分析通量背景对商几何的依赖，并将基态扇区简并度归因于作用在plaquette通量与不可收缩Wilson圈上的物理格点对称性；最后研究有限尺寸双vison能隙与物质费米子关联。

Result: isotropic 点下识别出基态超选择扇区，其相对均匀π通量的偏离随所选商的几何而变化；扇区简并完全由物理格点对称性（同时作用于plaquette通量和不可收缩Wilson圈）产生；有限尺寸双vison能隙随体系增大并不单调减小，而物质费米子关联在isotropic点仍呈预期的代数幂律衰减，即便在中等体系尺寸下也可观测到。

Conclusion: 三角形群有限商提供了在双曲格子上实现周期边界条件与拓扑扇区分析的可行且自洽的数值方案；有限双曲Kitaev模型在isotropic点保持自旋液相的关键特征（拓扑简并由格点对称性生成、关联的代数衰减），但双vison能隙的有限尺寸行为非单调，提示需谨慎进行外推。

Abstract: We construct and solve the three-colored Kitaev model on finite hyperbolic lattices with periodic boundary conditions. We describe a self-contained procedure which uses finite quotients of orientation- preserving triangle groups to generate regular maps on compact genus-g surfaces. From these, one can extract their lattice connectivity, plaquette structure, homology cycles and Wilson sectors while implementing consistent boundary conditions. On the example of {8, 3} and {12, 3} lattices, we enumerate a selection of quotients with up to 72 vertices, which constitute the discrete steps in a finite-size scaling analysis. For the isotropic point, corresponding to the spin-liquid phase in the thermodynamic limit, we identify the ground state superselection sectors, quantify their deviation from the homogeneous π flux sector and show how the selected flux background depends on the quotient geometry. We then discuss how the resulting ground state sector degeneracies are entirely generated by physical lattice symmetries acting on both the plaquette fluxes and non-contractible Wilson loops. Finally, we study the behaviour of the finite-size two-vison gap and matter fermion correlations. We find that the gap does not decrease monotonically with increasing system size, while one still recovers the expected algebraic decay of the correlations at the isotropic point, even for the considered modest system sizes.

</details>


### [116] [Magnon-Phonon-Polariton Optomechanics in a van der Waals Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21869)
*Jens-Christian Drawer,Vladislav Yu. Shishkov,Bo Han,Vita Solovyeva,Iva Plutnarová,Zdeněk Sofer,Christian Schneider,Anton V. Zasedatelev,Martin Esmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在悬浮CrSBr膜中研究自杂化激子极化激元、相干磁振子与呼吸型声学声子的三体光力学耦合，测得吸引型磁振子-声子耦合强度V/2π = -43 MHz，并观察到共振下磁振子到声子的单向能量转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状反铁磁体CrSBr中的自杂化激子极化激元可与相干磁振子和声子参量耦合，但三者之间的三体光力学相互作用尚缺乏实验研究，尤其是GHz频段下磁振子与声子耦合的方向性与强度。

Method: 采用支撑高Q呼吸型声学声子模式的悬浮CrSBr膜，对极化激元态进行共振泵浦-探测光谱测量，在约20 GHz范围解析耦合动力学；通过外加磁场扫过磁振子模式使其跨越声子共振。

Result: 观测到吸引型磁振子-声子相互作用，强度V/2π = -43 MHz，主要导致声子放大；在磁振子-声子共振处，能量流以磁振子向声子的单向转移为主，反向作用受相干磁振子的动力学损耗限制。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了层状范德华材料中集体磁激发与声、光的直接耦合界面，为GHz频段、具光学读出的量子相干换能铺平道路。

Abstract: The layered anti-ferromagnet CrSBr hosts self-hybridized exciton-polaritons that couple parametrically to coherent magnons and phonons. In this work, we explore the tripartite optomechanical interaction between these quasiparticles in suspended CrSBr membranes supporting high-$Q$ breathing-type acoustic phonon modes. Using resonant pump-probe spectroscopy on the polariton state, we resolve the coupled dynamics in the $\simeq 20$ GHz range while sweeping the magnon mode across the phonon resonance with an external magnetic field. We observe an attractive magnon-phonon interaction of strength $V/2π= -43$ MHz, which primarily leads to phonon amplification. At the magnon-phonon resonance, we find predominantly unidirectional energy flow from magnons to phonons while the possible back-action is limited by kinetic losses of coherent magnons. Our study enables direct interfacing of collective magnetic excitations with sound and light in layered van der Waals materials, paving the way for quantum-coherent transduction in the GHz range with optical readout.

</details>


### [117] [Complex mobility edges from competing localization channels in non-Hermitian quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22336)
*Bing Shao,Weiwei Zhu,Longwen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出含非对称跃迁与复在位势的GPD扩展模型，用Avila全局理论给出精确Lyapunov指数，揭示两个能量依赖局域化通道的竞争决定迁移边结构，并给出区分真实迁移边与失效解析候选的选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米局域化转变常伴随多重迁移边，但其物理起源存在歧义，缺乏统一的判别与描述框架。

Method: 将Ganeshan-Pixley-Das Sarma模型扩展到同时含非对称跃迁与复在位势的情形，覆盖广泛非厄米准周期链；借助Avila全局理论解析求得精确Lyapunov指数，在复能量平面上确定两个局域化通道的阈值，建立选择定则，并结合数值谱与有限尺寸局域化诊断验证。

Result: 发现两个具有不同Lyapunov增长率尺度的能量依赖局域化贡献，其竞争决定迁移边结构；唯一主导局域化的通道可随能量切换，产生非厄米起源的复杂迁移边；非互易跃迁会移动阈值并改变体能谱图样；数值结果与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为竞争局域化机制下的非厄米准晶相变与迁移边刻画提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian localization transitions often accompany multiple mobility edges, but their physical origin can be ambiguous. To address the issue, we introduce an extension of the Ganeshan-Pixley-Das Sarma model with both asymmetric hopping and complex onsite potential, thereby incorporating a broad range of non-Hermitian quasiperiodic chains. Its solution reveals two energy-dependent localization contributions manifested as distinct Lyapunov growth scales, whose competition determines the structure of mobility edges. We derive exact Lyapunov exponents via Avila's global theory, obtain thresholds of the two channels on complex-energy plane, and formulate a selection rule that distinguishes physical mobility edges from suppressed analytical candidates. The channel that uniquely controls localization can switch with energy, producing complex mobility edges of non-Hermitian origins. Nonreciprocal hopping further shifts the thresholds and alters the bulk energy patterns. Numerical spectra and finite-size localization diagnostics confirm the theory. Our work thus provides a unified framework for characterizing phase transitions and mobility edges under competing localization mechanisms in non-Hermitian quasicrystals.

</details>


### [118] [Optical characterization of Fermi polarons in monolayer MoSe$_2$ encapsulated in amorphous silicon nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21931)
*Christos Paspalides,Brendan C. Mulkerin,Rafael Luque Merino,Alvaro Castilla Lamas,Samuel Palacios Valdepeñas,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jesper Levinsen,Meera M. Parish,Luis Viña,Maria Dolores Martín,Francesca Maria Marchetti,Andres Castellanos-Gomez,Carlos Antón-Solanas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文比较了非晶氮化硅（SiN）与六方氮化硼（hBN）封装下单层MoSe₂的光学响应差异，指出SiN封装会引入显著电子掺杂，使中性激子响应转变为费米极化子（吸引/排斥支）响应，并用有限温费米极化子理论定量重现了实验反射与发光谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶氮化硅是晶圆级集成光子学的低损耗CMOS兼容平台，将TMD单层封装其中是实现可扩展TMD-光子电路的可行路径；但该非晶介电层如何影响单层的光学响应尚不清楚，因此需要定量研究封装介质诱导的载流子掺杂与激子响应变化。

Method: 制备SiN封装与hBN封装的单层MoSe₂样品，采用微分反射率与光致发光谱进行对比；将温度依赖反射谱与微观有限温费米极化子理论做定量拟合，提取电子密度，并以该密度与三激子结合能为唯一输入参数计算PL谱进行验证。

Result: hBN封装参考样品以中性激子为主；SiN封装单层出现相距约31 meV的吸引与排斥极化子共振，发光由吸引支主导。理论拟合给出SiN诱导的电子密度为(0.8–0.9)×10¹² cm⁻²；仅用该密度与三激子结合能两个参数，理论在至约80 K范围内定量重现PL谱，与实验高度一致。

Conclusion: 非晶氮化硅封装会向MoSe₂单层引入显著n型掺杂，其激子响应可由费米极化子图像完整描述；这为SiN平台上TMD-光子集成器件的光学设计与建模提供了定量依据，也提示封装介电层掺杂效应须纳入器件考量。

Abstract: Amorphous silicon nitride is the leading low-loss, CMOS-compatible platform for wafer-scale integrated photonics. Encapsulating transition-metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayers within this platform offers a promising route towards scalable TMD-photonic circuits. Yet the effect of this amorphous dielectric on the optical response of the monolayer remains poorly understood. Here, we compare monolayer MoSe$_2$ encapsulated in amorphous silicon nitride with a hexagonal boron nitride-encapsulated reference, using differential reflectivity and photoluminescence. Whereas the reference is dominated by the neutral exciton, the nitride-encapsulated monolayer shows attractive and repulsive polaron resonances separated by ${\sim}31$~meV, with emission dominated by the attractive branch. A quantitative comparison of temperature-dependent reflectance with a microscopic finite-temperature Fermi-polaron theory shows that the nitride induces substantial electron doping in the monolayer, with an electron density of $(0.8$--$0.9)\times10^{12}$~cm$^{-2}$. Using the extracted density and trion binding energy as the only input parameters, the theory reproduces the photoluminescence spectra up to ${\sim}80$ K in excellent quantitative agreement with experiments, thus corroborating the Fermi-polaron description of the observed excitonic response.

</details>


### [119] [Reconfigurable 2D Lateral Heterostructure Platform for Unified Detection, Emission, and Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22170)
*Purbasha Ray,Rupali Verma,Biswajeet Nayak,Suman Kumar Chakraborty,Shailendra K Varshney,Mayank Shrivastava,Prasana Kumar Sahoo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作演示了双层MoSe2-WSe2侧向异质结，在同一平台上集成光电探测、可重构电致发光与CMOS逻辑运算，均源于同一栅控耗尽区的静电框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感、发光、通信与计算的异质集成通常依赖专用器件，导致互连复杂、制备开销大、寄生损耗与能耗惩罚；亟需单片可重构器件统一调控载流子的产生、输运与复合。

Method: 构建双层MoSe2-WSe2侧向异质结，利用空间上电荷极性不同的畴区与可动态调控的静电势景观；通过栅压调控耗尽区位置，统一控制载流子抽取、复合与输运。

Result: 光电探测器响应度35 A W⁻¹、比探测率5×10¹³ Jones、噪声等效功率6.3×10⁻¹⁸ W Hz⁻¹ᐟ²；电注入下栅压可在MoSe2区、侧向异质界面与WSe2区之间切换辐射复合区，实现空间可编程EL、光学复用与三值光学逻辑；互补输运特性支持0.25 kHz CMOS反相器工作。

Conclusion: 2D侧向异质结可作为单片集成电学与光电子系统的多功能构建单元，集光感知、光产生与信息处理于一体。

Abstract: Heterogeneous integration of sensing, light generation, communication, and computation typically requires specialized devices, resulting in complex interconnects, fabrication overhead, parasitic losses, and energy penalties. Developing a monolithic, reconfigurable device capable of controlling carrier generation, transport, and recombination remains a longstanding challenge. Two-dimensional (2D) lateral heterostructures offer a promising approach by combining spatially distinct domains of different charge polarity with a dynamically tunable electrostatic landscape. Here, multifunctional bilayer MoSe2-WSe2 lateral heterojunctions are demonstrated, integrating photodetection, reconfigurable electroluminescence (EL), and CMOS logic operation within a unified platform. The photodetector exhibits a responsivity of 35 A W-1, a specific detectivity of 5 x 10^13 Jones, and a noise-equivalent power of 6.3 x 10^-18 W Hz^-1/2, enabled by efficient charge separation and photoconductive gain. Under electrical injection, electrostatic gating dynamically switches the radiative recombination zone among the MoSe2 domain, lateral heterointerface, and WSe2 domain, enabling spatially programmable EL, optical multiplexing, and ternary optical logic. Furthermore, the complementary transport characteristics of the two domains enable CMOS inverter operation at 0.25 kHz. These functionalities originate from a common electrostatic framework in which the gate-tunable depletion region governs carrier extraction, recombination, and transport. This work establishes 2D lateral heterostructures as multifunctional building blocks for monolithically integrated electronic and optoelectronic systems combining light sensing, generation, and information processing.

</details>


### [120] [Intrinsic second-order nonlinear Hall effect from perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22229)
*Zhi-Hao Wei,Xiao-Bin Qiang,Renjie Tong,Hai-Zhou Lu,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个教科书式的微扰理论，用于计算本征非线性霍尔电导率，并讨论了其与波包方法的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性输运是探测量子材料电子结构与对称性的有效手段，其中本征非线性霍尔效应不依赖杂质散射、直接反映布洛赫电子的量子几何，因而备受关注；但此前缺乏一个简洁、教科书式的推导框架。

Method: 以良定义的布洛赫态速度为出发点，通过微扰论分别确定电场修正后的速度与分布函数，再由二者直接得到非线性电导率；并将该框架与基于波包的理论进行对比。

Result: 给出了本征非线性电导率的简洁微扰表达式，并论证了该结果与波包理论相互一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为本征非线性霍尔效应提供了统一、直观且自洽的理论表述，有助于理解其量子几何起源。

Abstract: Nonlinear transport provides an efficient probe of the electronic structure and symmetry of quantum materials. As a prominent example, the intrinsic nonlinear Hall effect has attracted considerable attention in recent years because it is independent of impurity scattering and directly reflects the quantum geometry of Bloch electrons. Here we establish a simple textbook-style perturbation theory for the intrinsic nonlinear conductivity. Based on the well-defined Bloch-state velocity, our formulation perturbatively determines the field-corrected velocity and distribution function, from which the conductivity follows directly. We further compare our approach with wavepacket-based theories and discuss their consistency.

</details>


### [121] [Landscape index and its boundary correspondence in inhomogeneous chiral systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22500)
*Guo-Jian Qiao,Zhi-Lei Zhang,C. P. Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于手性体系零能本征方程空间动力学二次曲面几何的"景观指数"实数空间拓扑指标，其跨界面差值等于受保护的手性零模净数，并给出不依赖具体空间剖面的参数界，可经维度约化推广至高维。


<details>
