  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统体-边对应通常依赖渐近均匀的体区与布洛赫动量空间的卷绕数，对空间强烈非均匀、无均匀体区的界面体系缺乏直接、定量的实数空间指标与保护判据。

Method: 由手性系统零能本征方程构造空间动力学的二次型"景观"几何，据此定义实数空间景观指数；证明其在保持衰减解与增长解一致指数分离的连续变形下不变，并在均匀极限下等于连续卷绕数；通过维度约化将指数及其边界对应推广至高维，并给出哈密顿量参数及其空间梯度满足的解析界。

Result: 非均匀界面两侧景观指数之差等于受保护的手性零模净数，无需渐近均匀体区；解析界可对所有满足界的剖面确定指数而不需其具体空间形式；该框架保证任意横向余维的Kitaev型体系存在受保护径向零模，并刻画周期调制量子反常霍尔体系中支持手性边缘态的参数与动量区域；准周期界面下两側持续调制仍保证单一受保护手性分支，数值计算予以确认。

Conclusion: 景观几何将实数空间拓扑与边界态的受保护净手性联系起来，为无均匀体区和非周期界面的拓扑保护提供定量判据。

Abstract: We define a real-space topological index from the geometry of a quadratic landscape associated with the spatial dynamics, which is governed by the zero-energy eigenvalue equation of chiral systems. The landscape index remains invariant under continuous deformations preserving the uniform exponential separation between decaying and growing spatial solutions and equals the continuum winding number in the homogeneous limit. Across an inhomogeneous interface, the difference between the indices on the two sides equals the protected net chiral zero-mode count, without requiring asymptotically homogeneous bulk regions. Explicit landscape constructions yield analytical bounds on Hamiltonian parameters and their spatial gradients that determine the indices for all profiles satisfying these bounds, without specifying their detailed spatial form. Dimensional reduction extends the landscape index and its boundary correspondence to higher-dimensional systems. The resulting bounds guarantee protected radial zero modes in effective Kitaev-type systems of arbitrary transverse codimension and identify parameter and momentum regions supporting chiral edge states in periodically modulated quantum anomalous Hall systems. For the quasiperiodic interface considered, the index difference guarantees a single protected chiral branch despite persistent modulation on both sides, as confirmed by numerical calculations. Landscape geometry thus connects real-space topology to the protected net chirality of boundary states.

</details>


### [122] [Performance limits and benchmarking of ideal 2D topological insulator field-effect transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22779)
*Yosep Park,Jaeyoung Eom,Yeonghun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文建立了理想拓扑绝缘体场效应晶体管（TIFET）的性能极限，并在等效、真实条件下将其开关能量-延迟性能与硅基FET（FinFET、LGAA）及二维材料FET（MoS₂、InSe）进行系统基准对比，指出TIFET更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅基FET逼近微缩极限，亟需新型器件方案；TIFET被视为有潜力的替代者，但缺乏与现有器件的定量、同等条件下的性能对比。

Method: 建立理想TIFET的性能极限模型，并在等效与真实条件下与硅基FET（FinFET、LGAA）和二维材料FET（MoS₂、InSe）进行开关能量-延迟（energy-delay）基准评测；同时分析接触电阻R_C、寄生电容C_par、沟道长度L与沟道宽度W等关键因素的影响。

Result: TIFET实现显著更低的开关能量与延迟，优于硅基及二维材料FET；由1-stack增至3-stack可带来约6倍的性能改善（开关能量-延迟降低）。

Conclusion: TIFET具备有竞争力的开关性能，在未来拓扑电子器件中具有较大潜力；接触电阻、寄生电容与沟道尺寸是影响其能量-延迟表现的关键设计参数。

Abstract: Topological insulator field-effect transistors (TIFETs) have emerged as a promising alternative to overcome the scaling limits of silicon-based field-effect transistors (Si-based FETs). In this study, we establish the performance limits of ideal TIFETs and present a comprehensive benchmarking of the switching energy-delay performance of TIFETs against Si-based FETs (FinFET and LGAA) and 2-D material-based FETs ($\mathrm{MoS}_2$ and InSe) under equivalent and realistic conditions. Furthermore, we analyze the key factors influencing the switching energy-delay performance of TIFETs, including contact resistance ($R_{\mathrm{C}}$), parasitic capacitance ($C_{\mathrm{par}}$), channel length ($L$), and channel width ($W$). Notably, TIFETs achieve substantially lower switching energy and delay time, outperforming both Si-based FETs and 2-D material-based FETs, where a transition from 1-stack to 3-stack yields approximately a sixfold reduction. This benchmarking study shows that TIFETs exhibit competitive switching performance and hold strong potential for future topological electronic devices.

</details>


### [123] [Axion electrodynamics and the advection-diffusion structure of TEM modes in hollow-conductor waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22839)
*André H. Gomes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作系统研究了填充轴子电动力学介质的中空导体波导中的TEM模式，证明其横向静电势满足对流-扩散方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规单导体波导中TEM模式无法传播，而轴子场与外磁场的耦合可能使其成为可能，作者希望厘清其物理机制并建立与输运现象的数学联系。

Method: 基于轴子电动力学框架推导中空波导中的TEM模式方程，导出横向静电势的对流-扩散型方程；对圆柱波导给出解析解，分别考虑均匀漂移场、刚性旋转漂移场及二者叠加，并考察不同Péclet数下的场分布。

Result: TEM模式由对流-扩散方程支配，明确建立了轴子电动力学与对流-扩散现象的直接数学对应；圆柱波导的显式解与场剖面揭示背景空间变化如何塑造横向电磁场，且Péclet数（对流/扩散比）调控场结构。

Conclusion: 提出了以输运为核心的框架，用以理解轴子电动力学背景的空间变化如何影响中空导体波导中的横向电磁场。

Abstract: We present a systematic investigation of transverse electromagnetic (TEM) modes in hollow-conductor waveguides filled with a dielectric medium described by axion electrodynamics. While such modes do not propagate in conventional single-conductor waveguides, the coupling of the axion field to an external magnetic field enables their propagation. We show that the resulting TEM modes are governed by an advection-diffusion-type equation for the transverse electrostatic potential, establishing a direct mathematical connection between axion electrodynamics and advection-diffusion phenomena. We illustrate this connection through explicit solutions and field profiles for cylindrical waveguides, considering uniform and rigid rotational drift fields, both individually and in combination, as well as different values of the parameter controlling the advection-to-diffusion ratio (Péclet number). These results establish a transport-based framework for understanding how spatial variations of the axion-electrodynamics background shape the transverse electromagnetic fields in hollow-conductor waveguides.

</details>


### [124] [Electrical addressing of compensated magnetic order in artificial spin ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22912)
*Stéphane Nilsson,Lauren J. Riddiford,Emily Darwin,Hans J. Hug,Aleš Hrabec,Laura J. Heyderman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用三角形人工自旋冰作为介观平台，借自旋轨道矩的取向依赖实现三个子晶格独立电写、平面霍尔效应实现子晶格分辨电读，从而电学写入与读取完全补偿态，并观察到非线性、历史依赖的电学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补偿磁体（如反铁磁体）因杂散场消失、抗扰动、超快动力学而适合自旋电子器件，但其序参量的电学写入与读取仍是主要难题；作者需要可工程化、可成像、可电控的介观模型平台。

Method: 以三角形人工自旋冰为平台：利用自旋轨道矩翻转电流的取向依赖性，通过单一电流通道独立翻转三个纳米磁子晶格；以平面霍尔效应对子晶格进行分辨读出，从而寻址三角形等边三磁体构型的全部八种磁化状态。

Result: 实现了对包含完全补偿态在内的八种磁化状态的访问，并观测到非线性、依赖历史（记忆性）的电学响应，实现补偿态的电学写入与读取。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了电学可编程补偿磁体系统的途径，为可重构磁性器件与非传统计算奠定基础。

Abstract: Compensated magnetic systems, such as antiferromagnets, hold immense potential for spintronic devices thanks to vanishing stray fields, robustness to perturbations, and ultrafast dynamics. Yet, electrical writing and reading of their order remains a major challenge. Here we use artificial spin ice as a mesoscale platform in which compensated order can be engineered, directly imaged, and electrically controlled. Specifically, we demonstrate combined electrical writing and reading of a compensated state in a triangular artificial spin ice. Exploiting the orientation dependence of the spin-orbit torque switching current, we independently switch three nanomagnet sublattices through a single current channel, with the planar Hall effect providing sublattice-resolved readout. This grants access to all eight magnetization states of the triangular plaquette, including its fully compensated state, yielding a nonlinear, history-dependent electrical response. Our results establish an approach to electrically programmable compensated magnetic systems and provide an important foundation for reconfigurable magnetic devices and unconventional computing.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamics of a Single-Domain Dissipative Magnetic Gyroscope in Electric and Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23167)
*Y. B. Band,Igor Kuzmenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立单畴对称陀螺铁磁针（磁性陀螺）在静电磁场中的自旋-转动耦合动力学模型，用无奇点四元数运动学求解并数值模拟120 nm钴针，揭示自旋反转、章动型进动与电场极化力矩效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将铁磁针同时作为机械转子和自旋载体，其自旋与转动的耦合（含磁电耦合、相对论运动耦合、导电针诱导偶极的Debye弛豫耗散）缺乏完整动力学描述；同时需评估电场对导电针的干扰，判断其能否作为磁强计。

Method: 构建包含转动动能、磁晶与形状各向异性、Zeeman能、导电针诱导偶极静电能、线性磁电耦合及相对论运动（自旋-轨道）耦合的保守哈密顿量；引入Gilbert阻尼与Debye弛豫耗散；在实验室系与随体系给出方程，采用无奇点四元数运动学；对120 nm单畴钴针在0.5 T场中数值求解。

Result: (i) 各向异性场约1.3 T大于外加场，初始Gilbert阻尼瞬态中自旋多次反向，反转次数奇偶决定易轴最终平行或反平行于自旋；(ii) 瞬态后针绕场做天平动（进动伴以二倍频章动），周期约20 ns，与2π(I₁/μB)^{1/2}一致，Zeeman能与转动动能反相振荡而总能量守恒；(iii) 静电场通过形状相关极化力矩使长轴趋向E，磁电耦合与运动磁场（≲1 μT）力矩可忽略，故仅在E≪E_c时可用作磁强计。

Conclusion: 导电铁磁针的动力学由形状各向异性、Gilbert阻尼与电场极化力矩主导；磁电与运动耦合项贡献可忽略。该体系可作为磁强计的前提是电场远小于临界场E_c，否则极化力矩会主导取向。

Abstract: We formulate the coupled spin--rotation dynamics of a single-domain symmetric top ferromagnetic needle (a magnetic gyroscope) in static magnetic and electric fields. The conservative Hamiltonian contains the rotational kinetic energy, the magnetocrystalline and shape anisotropy, the Zeeman energy, the electrostatic energy of the dipole induced in the conducting needle, the linear magnetoelectric coupling, and the relativistic motional (spin-orbit) coupling. Gilbert damping is introduced, and in an electric field, Debye relaxation of the induced electric dipole of the conducting needle also results in dissipation. We give the equations of motion in the laboratory and body-fixed frames, with singularity-free quaternion kinematics. For a 120-nm-long single-domain cobalt needle in a 0.5~T field we find: (i) because the anisotropy field ($\approx 1.3$~T) exceeds the applied magnetic field, the spin reverses direction several times during the initial Gilbert-damping transient, and the parity of the number of reversals determines whether the easy axis ends up parallel or antiparallel to the spin; (ii) after the transient the needle librates about the field---precession accompanied by nutation at twice the precession frequency---with a period of about 20~ns, consistent with the libration period $2π(I_1/μB)^{1/2}$, while the Zeeman and rotational kinetic energies oscillate out of phase and the total energy is conserved; (iii) a static electric field $\mathbf E$ exerts on the conducting needle a shape-dependent polarization torque that tends to align the long axis of the needle with $\mathbf E$. The torques due to the magnetoelectric interaction and motional magnetic fields ($\lesssim1~μ$T) are negligible compared with the polarization torque. A conducting needle can therefore serve as a magnetometer only if $E\ll E_c$.

</details>


### [126] [Photon condensation driven by scalar spin chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23178)
*Dimitrije Stepanenko,Daniel Loss,Mircea Trif*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出三角分子磁体与腔耦合可在平衡态实现由标量自旋手性驱动的光子凝聚，突破规范不变性对偶极耦合的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范不变性禁止空间均匀电偶极耦合下的平衡光子凝聚，需寻找基于轨道磁响应的替代路径。

Method: 从Mott区半满Hubbard模型出发，导出虚拟环交换使腔磁通直接耦合标量自旋手性的有效哈密顿量；结合平均场理论、集体涨落计算与有限尺寸精确对角化。

Result: 发现自发手性有序与宏观腔位移共存的新相，并存在手性-光子杂化激发；凝聚阈值可通过分子手性劈裂电学调控。

Conclusion: 确立阻挫磁体中涌现轨道自由度作为超越常规偶极腔QED的平衡光-物质有序新途径。

Abstract: Gauge invariance forbids equilibrium photon condensation for spatially uniform electric-dipole coupling, motivating alternative routes based on orbital magnetic response. We show that triangular molecular magnets coupled to a cavity support photon condensation driven by emergent scalar spin chirality. Starting from the half-filled Hubbard model in the Mott regime, we derive an effective Hamiltonian in which virtual ring exchange couples the cavity flux directly to scalar spin chirality. Mean-field theory, collective-fluctuation calculations, and finite-size exact diagonalization reveal a phase with spontaneous chirality ordering and a macroscopic cavity displacement, together with hybrid chirality-photon excitations. The condensation threshold can be tuned electrically through the molecular chirality splitting. Our results establish emergent orbital degrees of freedom in frustrated magnets as a route to equilibrium light-matter ordering beyond conventional dipolar cavity quantum electrodynamics.

</details>

### [127] [Few Dipolar Bosons in a Ring Trap: Quantum States and Transitions between Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22562)
*Jieli Qin*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文用精确对角化方法研究环形势中少体偶极玻色气体，揭示Wigner晶体、超流、量子液滴及其混合态的丰富相图与转变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简并偶极玻色气体中短程接触相互作用与长程偶极-偶极相互作用的竞争可产生新奇量子态。既有研究多基于扩展平均场理论，依赖Lee-Huang-Yang修正近似处理量子涨落，难以刻画少体、强关联情形下的完整量子特性。

Method: 考虑囚禁于环形（ring）势中的少体偶极玻色子体系，采用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）方法求解，获得系统的全部量子信息；通过分析量子关联（如密度关联、二阶关联函数）与相干性质来识别不同量子态。

Result: 识别出包括Wigner晶体、超流态、量子液滴以及它们混合态在内的多种量子态，并阐明各自的形成机制；进一步证明通过调节相互作用强度与粒子数可实现这些量子态之间的转变。

Conclusion: 该工作从微观量子层面加深了对少体偶极气体的理解，为偶极气体在量子传感与量子信息等方向的应用提供了参考。

Abstract: The degenerate dipolar Bose gas has attracted great research interest for studying novel quantum states of matter due to the interplay between short-range contact and long-range dipole-dipole interactions. The superfluid, quantum droplet, and supersolid states, along with the transition between them, have been widely studied using the extended mean-field theory, which relies on the Lee-Huang-Yang correction to approximately account for the effect of quantum fluctuation. In this work, we examine the system of a few dipolar bosons trapped in a ring potential employing the exact diagonalization method, which can yield the full quantum nature of the system. By examining the quantum correlation and coherence properties of the system, we reveal a rich variety of quantum states (including Wigner crystal, superfluid, quantum droplet, and the mixed states of them) and their formation mechanisms. Furthermore, we show that transitions between these quantum states can be induced by tuning the interaction strength and particle number. The findings of this work can provide valuable insights into the dipolar gas at the microscopic quantum level, and may also find applications in the fields such as quantum sensing and quantum information.

</details>


### [128] [Specific heat in the BCS-rashbon BEC crossover regime of an ultracold Fermi gas with a 3D spherical spin-orbit coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23121)
*Yusuke Okajima,Koki Takemoto,Shumpei Iwasaki,Yoji Ohashi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在Nozières–Schmitt-Rink强耦合理论框架内计入自旋轨道耦合下的配对涨落，计算定容比热C_V，以刻画费米气体—rashbon气体的演化并统一描述BCS–BEC与BCS–rashbon BEC两类渡越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可调球对称自旋轨道耦合（SOC）的超冷费米气体中，即使配对相互作用本身不足以形成双体束缚态，也能形成名为rashbon的双体束缚态；需要一个敏感的热力学量来刻画系统随SOC强度的性质变化。

Method: 在Nozières–Schmitt-Rink（NSR）强耦合理论框架内，计入SOC存在下的配对涨落，计算定容比热C_V，并分析其在T_c处及正常态的温度依赖关系。

Result: 在超流相变温度T_c处，C_V在弱耦合费米气体区与强SOC rashbon气体区之间的中等SOC区域出现峰值结构；在T_c以上正常态，通过C_V的温度依赖分析识别出三类区域：(1) 费米原子气体主导、(2) 稳定rashbon玻色气体主导、(3) rashbon涨落主导。

Conclusion: 定容比热C_V是区分费米气体、rashbon气体及其涨落区域的有效探针；结合通过调节配对相互作用实现的传统BCS–BEC渡越，给出了BCS–BEC与BCS–rashbon BEC渡越现象的统一描述。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate strong-coupling properties of an ultracold Fermi gas with a tunable spherical (isotropic) spin-orbit interaction. In this spin-orbit coupled system, it is known that a two-body bound state, called rashbon, can be formed even in the coupling regime where a pairing interaction alone cannot form such a two-body bound molecule. In this paper, we evaluate the specific heat at constant volume $C_V$, including pairing fluctuations in the presence of SOC within the framework of the strong-coupling theory developed by Nozières and Schmitt-Rink, to show that this thermodynamic quantity is very useful to see how system properties vary from a Fermi gas to a rashbon gas with increasing spin-orbit coupling strength. At the superfluid phase transition temperature $T_{\rm c}$, $C_V$ is found to exhibit a peak structure in the intermediate spin-orbit coupling regime between the weak-coupling Fermi gas regime and a strong-SOC rashbon gas regime. In the normal state above $T_{\rm c}$, from detailed analyses of the temperature dependences of $C_V$, we also identify the regions where system properties are dominated by (1) a Fermi atomic gas, (2) a Bose gas of stable rashbons, and (3) fluctuating rashbons. Combining these results with the conventional Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS)--Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) crossover physics realized by tuning the strength of a pairing interaction, we present a unified description of the BCS--BEC and BCS--rashbon BEC crossover phenomena.

</details>


### [129] [Richtmyer-Meshkov instability induced by a Gaussian-kicked rotor-like potential in a three-component Bose-Einstein condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23171)
*Susovan Giri*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在三组分BEC中通过kick势模拟冲击波触发Richtmyer-Meshkov不稳定性，数值研究其非线性演化和量子湍流能谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多组分超流体系中Richtmyer-Meshkov不稳定性的触发机制、涡旋产生与非平衡斑图演化，弥补三组分BEC中该类流体力学不稳定性研究的不足。

Method: 提出kick势（kick rotor模型）在三组分BEC的两个界面上模拟脉冲激波；基于流体静压方程建立流体力学不稳定性判据的水动力学模型；采用分裂步Crank-Nicolson方法数值求解耦合Gross-Pitaevskii方程，在准二维谐振阱中获得各组分的互不混溶基态，再于两界面引入类高斯扰动追踪非线性斑图的时演。

Result: 界面演化为量子湍流与非线性结构；不可压缩与可压缩分量的动能谱在低波数区分别呈-5/3与1幂律，在高波数区分别为k^-4与k^-7/2，与已知量子湍流谱特征一致。

Conclusion: 三组分BEC可作为研究Richtmyer-Meshkov不稳定性、涡旋生成与量子湍流的可控平台，所给能谱标度验证了多组分超流中湍流谱的普适性。

Abstract: This research investigates the Richtmyer-Meshkov instability within a three-component Bose-Einstein condensate. We offer a kicking potential that replicates the impulsive shock across both interfaces of the three-component BEC to trigger the Richtmyer-Meshkov instability. We propose a hydrodynamic model that indicates the essential criteria for the onset of hydrodynamic instability in the BEC through the application of the hydrodynamic pressure equation. We utilise the split-step Crank-Nicolson method to numerically solve the coupled Gross-Pitaevskii equations, with the objective of obtaining the immiscible ground state of all components in a quasi-2D harmonic trap. Following this, we introduce a Gaussian-like disruption instigated by the kick rotor model at both interfaces of the confined three-component BEC to explore the time-dependent development of nonlinear hydrodynamic instability patterns, culminating in quantum turbulence and nonlinear configurations at the interfaces. The kinetic energy spectra of both the incompressible and compressible elements of the system exhibit scaling in the low-wavenumber domain with power-law exponents of $-5/3$ and $1$, respectively. Conversely, the high-wavenumber domain is defined by $k^{-4}$ and $k^{-7/2}$ scaling for the incompressible and compressible elements, respectively, aligning with the established spectral characteristics of quantum turbulence. This research offers fresh perspectives on the mechanisms of hydrodynamic instability, vortex generation, non-equilibrium pattern evolution, and quantum turbulence in superfluid systems comprising multiple interacting elements.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Contact-number hyperuniformity at glass close packing in infinite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22209)
*Harukuni Ikeda*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 用复本理论研究高维极限下玻璃密堆积(GCP)附近的接触数涨落，证明GCP处接触数呈超均匀性，弱压缩下低频平台高度正比于过剩配位数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，玻璃密堆积(GCP)被Gardner不稳定性区域包围，难以沿稳定分支解析地逼近；同时，接触数涨落（及其超均匀性）此前缺乏直接从微观相互作用出发的解析描述。

Method: 采用复本(replica)理论，分析Gardner不稳定区随空间维度增大而向堵塞点收缩的行为，证明高维极限下可沿稳定的单步复本对称破缺(1-RSB)分支逼近GCP；并通过将外场耦合到局域接触数，从微观相互作用直接推导总接触数方差与波数依赖的结构因子。

Result: 在GCP处，结构因子在小标度波数下呈二次方衰减，表明存在接触数超均匀性；当GCP以上发生弱压缩时，结构因子出现低频平台，其高度与过剩配位数成正比。

Conclusion: 给出了接触数超均匀性及其在压缩下被破坏的解析描述，表明高维极限下可沿稳定1-RSB分支逼近GCP，并建立了结构因子低频行为与过剩配位数之间的定量联系。

Abstract: We investigate contact-number fluctuations of harmonic spheres near glass close packing (GCP), the amorphous jamming endpoint reached by compressing an ideal glass. Using replica theory, we show that the Gardner-unstable region shrinks toward jamming as the spatial dimension increases, allowing GCP to be approached along a stable one-step replica symmetry breaking branch in the high-dimensional limit. By coupling an external field to the local contact number, we derive the variance of the total contact number and the wave-number-dependent structure factor directly from the microscopic interactions. At GCP, the structure factor vanishes quadratically at small scaled wave numbers, demonstrating contact-number hyperuniformity. Upon weak compression above GCP, the structure factor develops a low-wave-number plateau whose height is proportional to the excess coordination. These results provide an analytical description of contact-number hyperuniformity and its breakdown upon compression.

</details>


### [131] [Stochastic Dynamics of Domain Walls on a Racetrack: Impact of Inhomogeneity of Magnetic Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22460)
*Anton V. Hlushchenko,Mykhailo I. Bratchenko,Oksana L. Andrieieva,Kostyantyn I. Polozhiy,Andriy M. Styervoyedov,Igor M. Sokolov,Samudrajit Thapa,Stuart S. P. Parkin,Aleksei V. Chechkin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 通过二维分形Weierstrass–Mandelbrot函数建模各向异性不均匀性，微磁模拟揭示了无热涨落下电流驱动畴壁输运的随机性，并给出透射率的误差函数统计、普适标度律及畴壁位置方差的唯象模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赛道存储器依赖电流驱动磁畴壁实现信息存储，但超薄磁性薄膜中的原子尺度界面无序会造成各向异性的空间不均匀，形成复杂能量景观，显著影响畴壁运动并带来可靠性问题，需要定量刻画。

Method: 采用二维分形Weierstrass–Mandelbrot函数描述各向异性不均匀性，可独立调节无序振幅与特征空间尺度；在此基础上进行微磁学模拟，定义透射率为穿过无序赛道后成功到达探测器的畴壁比例，并系统研究其对电流密度、无序振幅与空间尺度的依赖，同时对畴壁轨迹作统计分析。

Result: 即使在无热涨落条件下，电流驱动畴壁输运仍表现出显著随机性；透射率可用高斯误差函数剖面描述，且以中位值归一化后呈现普适标度行为；畴壁位置方差强烈依赖无序振幅，并可由所提出的唯象模型良好描述。

Conclusion: 研究为结构无序赛道中随机畴壁输运提供了定量描述框架，并确立了无序诱导的赛道存储器件可靠性极限。

Abstract: Racetrack memory is a spintronic technology that stores information in current-driven magnetic domain walls (DW), offering a promising route toward non-volatile, high-density, and high-speed memory devices. In ultrathin magnetic films, atomic-scale interface disorder can produce spatially inhomogeneous magnetic anisotropy, creating a complex spatially varying energy landscape that strongly influences DW motion. In this work, we model anisotropy inhomogeneities using a two-dimensional fractal Weierstrass--Mandelbrot function, which provides independent control over the disorder amplitude and characteristic spatial scale. Micromagnetic simulations reveal pronounced stochasticity of current-driven domain-wall transport even in the absence of thermal fluctuations. Transport is quantified by the transmission fraction, defined as the fraction of DWs that successfully reach a detector after propagating through a disordered racetrack. We investigate its dependence on current density, disorder amplitude, and the spatial scale of the anisotropy inhomogeneity. The resulting transmission statistics are described by Gaussian error-function profiles and exhibit a universal scaling behavior upon normalization by the corresponding median values. Statistical analysis of DW trajectories further characterizes the disorder-induced fluctuations of the propagation process. The variance of the random DW positions shows a pronounced dependence on disorder amplitude and is well described by the proposed phenomenological model. These results provide a quantitative framework for describing stochastic DW transport in structurally disordered racetracks and establish disorder-induced reliability limits in racetrack-memory devices.

</details>
